Conclusion: 该工作确立了三维Navier-Stokes方程的一个单速度分量、定标临界、空间频率ℓ^1细化的正则性判据，同时不要求时间方向上的Lorentz细化。所发展的频率-尺度匹配与双侧ℓ^1核方法为后续研究提供了新的技术工具，具体"未细化的临界条件u^3∈L^p_t L^m_x"这一开放问题仍保留，但本文结果在其充分性方向上迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: We prove a scaling-critical regularity criterion involving only one velocity component for finite-energy suitable weak solutions of the three-dimensional incompressible Navier--Stokes equations. Let $2<p<\infty$ and $m=3p/(p-2)$, so that $2/p+3/m=1$. We show that a singularity cannot occur provided \[
  \sum_{j\in\mathbb Z}
  \|\dotΔ_j u^3\|_{L^p(0,T;L^m(\mathbb R^3))}<\infty, \] that is, $u^3\in\widetilde L^p(0,T;\dot B^0_{m,1}(\mathbb R^3))$. The assumption is a spatial-frequency $\ell^1$ refinement of the still unresolved critical condition $u^3\in L^p_tL^m_x$ and is complementary to the Lorentz-in-time refinement $L^{p,1}_tL^m_x$ obtained by Wang, Wu, and Zhang. By Bernstein embedding, the result extends to $u^3\in\widetilde L^p_t\dot B^s_{q,1}$ on the nonnegative-smoothness critical line $s=-1+2/p+3/q\ge0$.
  The principal innovation is a frequency--scale matching scheme embedded in the local energy inequality. Each dyadic block of $u^3$ is retained until it is paired with the vertical scale selected by a one-dimensional backward heat kernel. Low frequencies gain from the slab thickness, high frequencies from transferring the projection to the localized flux and applying an inverse Bernstein estimate, and spatially separated pressure sources from harmonic decay. These mechanisms generate a two-sided $\ell^1$ kernel, converting spatial-frequency summability into summability of physical-scale energy increments. Consequently, we obtain a uniform Type-I local energy bound without a Lorentz refinement in time; compactness and one-component rigidity then exclude singular blow-up limits whose third velocity component vanishes.

</details>

Abstract: We prove two anisotropic halfspace theorems for uniformly elliptic parametric integrands. The proof replaces the catenoid in the Hoffman--Meeks argument by a strict exterior graph modeled on an elliptic logarithm. In arbitrary dimension and without assuming evenness, every global anisotropic-perimeter minimizer whose nonempty boundary lies in a halfspace is itself a halfspace. The proof combines wall contact with a plane-peeling argument that turns any residual phase defect into a minimizer for the reversed integrand and excludes it by a second blow-down.

</details>

The proof combines a shifted Jacobi inequality for \(b=\log(H+J_0)\), a parallel hypersurface transformation that makes the Newton tensor uniformly elliptic and local boundedness estimate then reduces the pointwise bound to a weighted $L^1$ estimate, which is completed using the Jacobi energy inequality and integration by parts.

*Congming Li,Yugao Ouyang,Zixuan Wang*

TL;DR: 本文受Amrouche-Girault-Giroire（1994）关于经典Laplace方程加权Sobolev空间理论的启发，为分数阶Laplacian建立了对应的L^p理论：引入以||(-Δ)^{s/2}u||_{L^p}为最高阶度量的加权分数阶Sobolev空间Λ^{s,p}(R^n)，证明(-Δ)^{s/2}在商空间Λ^{s,p}(R^n)/P_{[s-n/p]}到L^p(R^n)上是同构，覆盖s∈(0,2)、p∈(1,∞)，并通过对偶推广到负阶数；作为应用，得到了分数阶Stokes系统的存在唯一性及显式核与相容条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典理论（Amrouche-Girault-Giroire，1994）通过加权Sobolev空间的同构解决了R^n上的Laplace方程，但分数阶Laplacian对应的L^p理论尚不完善。作者希望将该框架推广到分数阶情形，从而为分数阶Laplacian方程建立系统且完备的可解性理论，并服务于分数阶Stokes系统等应用。

Method: (1) 引入由||(-Δ)^{s/2}u||_{L^p}衡量的加权分数阶Sobolev空间Λ^{s,p}(R^n)；(2) 建立适应于此范数的加权Hardy与Poincaré不等式（1≤s<2），通过对梯度的归约证明；(3) 先在Schwartz类的闭包上证明同构，利用两个解的差为整的s-调和函数（从而为多项式）这一事实，证明该闭包与Λ^{s,p}(R^n)一致，密度作为副产品获得；(4) 通过对偶扩展至负阶数，使分数阶Laplacian在相应空间尺度上构成精确的同构与复合关系。

Result: 证明了(-Δ)^{s/2}:Λ^{s,p}(R^n)/P_{[s-n/p]}→L^p(R^n)对所有s∈(0,2)、p∈(1,∞)是同构，即方程(-Δ)^{s/2}u=f对每个f∈L^p(R^n)可解，解在显式有限维多项式空间模下唯一。通过负阶数的对偶扩展，建立了分数阶Laplacian在不同阶数空间之间的精确同构与复合。作为应用，获得了分数阶Stokes系统在R^n上的存在唯一性、显式核及相容条件。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将Amrouche-Girault-Giroire的经典加权Sobolev空间框架推广至分数阶Laplacian的L^p理论，建立了完整可解性与唯一性（模多项式空间），并展示了其对分数阶Stokes系统的适用性，为该方向提供了统一且自洽的泛函分析框架。

Abstract: A classical theory of Amrouche, Girault and Giroire (1994) resolves the Laplace equation on $\mathbb{R}^n$ through an isomorphism between weighted Sobolev spaces. Inspired by their framework, we develop the corresponding $L^p$ theory for the fractional Laplacian: we introduce weighted fractional Sobolev spaces $Λ^{s,p}(\mathbb{R}^n)$, gauged at top order by $\|(-\triangle)^{\frac{s}{2}}u\|_{L^p}$, and prove that $(-\triangle)^{\frac{s}{2}}:Λ^{s,p}(\mathbb{R}^n)/\mathcal P_{[s-n/p]}\to L^p(\mathbb{R}^n)$ is an isomorphism for all $s\in(0,2)$ and $p\in(1,\infty)$; thus $(-\triangle)^{\frac{s}{2}}u=f$ is solvable for every $f\in L^p(\mathbb{R}^n)$, uniquely modulo an explicit finite-dimensional space of polynomials. More generally, extending the scale by duality to negative orders, the fractional Laplacians of the appropriate orders map its spaces isomorphically onto one another and compose exactly. A key ingredient is a family of weighted Hardy and Poincaré inequalities adapted to $\|(-\triangle)^{\frac{s}{2}}u\|_{L^p}$, established here for $1\le s<2$ by a reduction to the gradient. Since these inequalities hold a priori only on a dense class, while the density of $C_{\mathrm{c}}^\infty(\mathbb{R}^n)$ in $Λ^{s,p}(\mathbb{R}^n)$ is itself nontrivial, we first prove the isomorphism on the closure of the Schwartz class, which coincides with $Λ^{s,p}(\mathbb{R}^n)$ since the difference of two solutions is an entire $s$-harmonic function, hence a polynomial; the density follows as a by-product. As an application, we obtain existence and uniqueness, with explicit kernels and compatibility conditions, for the fractional Stokes system in $\mathbb{R}^n$.

</details>

Result: 成功构造了精确的行波解，并且证明此前已发表的结果只是本文结果的特殊情况，即本文结果具有更广的适用范围。

Conclusion: 该研究推广了既有文献中的相关结果，为分支布朗运动投票模型的半线性抛物方程提供了更一般的精确行波解框架。

Abstract: We construct exact traveling-wave solutions for a semilinear parabolic equation with a specific nonlinearity. This nonlinearity arises, in particular, in voting models based on branching Brownian motion. Previously known results are special cases of those presented here.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究波-热耦合系统的长期行为（稳定性和衰减率），特别是在热方程具有空间依赖系数且定义域为Lipschitz区域的更具一般性设定下，建立不依赖于具体区域与系数的统一结论，并推广已有衰减率结果。

Method: 1) 利用closure relations框架，将耦合系统的谱分析约化为纯波方程的谱分析；2) 借助C₀-半群理论与状态空间的非正交分解，将经典解的衰减率问题化约为一个残差估计；该残差估计独立于热区域和热系数，仅单调依赖于波区域和界面。

Result: 建立了与区域和系数无关的强渐近稳定性；在无任何额外假设下得到对数衰减率，在一维情形或满足几何控制条件（GCC）时得到多项式衰减率，且这些结果均推广至空间依赖热系数情形。

Abstract: We study of the long-term behavior of a coupled wave-heat system. The system consists of a wave equation and a heat equation on two adjacent Lipschitz domains coupled by a common interface, with the heat equation being allowed to incorporate spatially dependant coefficients.
  We first establish strong asymptotic stability independent of the domains and coefficients. To this end, we employ the framework of closure relations, which reduces the spectral analysis of the coupled system to that of a wave equation.
  Secondly, we analyze non-uniform decay rates for classical solutions to the coupled system. Using methods from the theory of $C_0$-semigroups and a non-orthogonal decomposition of the state space, we reduce the problem to a residual estimate which is independent of the heat domain and heat coefficients, instead depending monotonically on the wave domain and the interface. With this, we are able to extend the known non-uniform decay rates to spatially dependent heat coefficients, yielding logarithmic decay under no assumptions, as well as polynomial decay in one dimensions or under the Geometric Control Condition.

</details>


### [94] [Caffarelli Estimates under Lipschitz Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04052)
*Maja Gwóźdź*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了Brenier映射在边际分布一阶扰动下的无维数微分估计。证明了当两个边际分布受到全局Lipschitz扰动时，Caffarelli收缩定理的结论仍然成立，无需额外假设，从而回答了Fathi、Mikulincer和Shenfeld提出的猜想。具体地，从标准高斯测度到其乘以e^{-B}形式的扰动测度的Brenier映射具有全局Lipschitz代表元，其界仅依赖于Lipschitz常数L，且Brenier势属于C^{1,1}并满足无维数双侧Hessian界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Caffarelli的收缩定理在源与目标势满足逐点Hessian比较时给出了Brenier映射的无维数微分估计。作者希望证明在任意全局Lipschitz扰动两个边际分布的情况下，相同的结论仍然成立，无需额外假设。这直接对应于Fathi、Mikulincer和Shenfeld的猜想，是Brenier映射理论中关于鲁棒性（扰动稳定性）的核心问题。

Method: 作者从各向异性的双边际结果出发推导高斯估计。设V,W为势函数，Q,P为正定矩阵，利用分布曲率界D^2V⪯Q和D^2W⪰P，建立任意全局Lipschitz扰动参考边际分布后Brenier映射的矩阵Hessian界。关键创新在于常数保持本质梯度范围的各向方向几何。对于仿射扰动，该估计恢复了尖端的非交换Caffarelli张量；同时进一步获得了高斯扰动的逐点位移界和紧高斯混合的光谱Hessian估计。上界常数满足log C(0,L)=4L^2+log L+O(1)（L→∞），而一维例子给出下界log Lip(T_L)≥L^2/2。

Result: 证明了高斯测度在任意全局Lipschitz扰动下的Brenier映射具有全局Lipschitz代表元，其Lipschitz常数仅依赖于L，Brenier势属于C^{1,1}(R^d)且满足无维数双侧Hessian界。给出了上界常数C(0,L)的渐近估计log C(0,L)=4L^2+log L+O(1)，并通过一维例子建立了下界log Lip(T_L)≥L^2/2（两者在L的指数主阶上一致）。各向异性双边际框架还恢复了仿射扰动的非交换Caffarelli张量，并获得高斯扰动的逐点位移界及紧高斯混合的光谱Hessian估计。

Conclusion: 作者回答了Fathi-Mikulincer-Shenfeld猜想：在无额外假设的情况下，任意全局Lipschitz扰动下Caffarelli收缩定理的结论仍成立。所建立的各向异性框架统一处理了多种扰动类型（高斯、仿射、紧高斯混合），其维数无关的常数精确追踪了方向几何信息，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: We study dimension-free differential estimates for Brenier maps under first-order perturbations of the two marginals. Caffarelli's contraction theorem yields such estimates when the source and target potentials satisfy a pointwise Hessian comparison. We show that the same conclusion remains true under arbitrary globally Lipschitz perturbations of both marginals, without additional assumptions. In particular, this answers the conjecture due to Fathi, Mikulincer, and Shenfeld.
  More precisely, let $d\ge1$, $L\ge0$, and let $B:\mathbb{R}^d\to\mathbb{R}$ be globally $L$-Lipschitz. We prove that the Brenier map from the standard Gaussian measure $γ_d$ to the probability measure proportional to $\mathrm{e}^{-B}γ_d$ has a globally Lipschitz representative whose bound depends only on $L$. The Brenier potential also belongs to $C^{1,1}(\mathbb{R}^d)$ and satisfies dimension-free two-sided Hessian bounds. Let $\mathfrak{C}(0,L)$ denote the upper-bound constant, then \[ \log\mathfrak{C}(0,L)=4L^2+\log L+\mathcal{O}(1) \qquad(L\to\infty), \] and a one-dimensional example with Brenier map $T_L$ satisfies $\log\operatorname{Lip}(T_L)\ge L^2/2$.
  We deduce this Gaussian estimate from an anisotropic two-marginal result. Let $V,W:\mathbb{R}^d\to\mathbb{R}$ be potentials, and let $Q,P$ be positive-definite matrices. Using the distributional curvature bounds $D^2V\preceq Q$ and $D^2W\succeq P$, we establish matrix Hessian bounds for Brenier maps between arbitrary globally Lipschitz perturbations of the reference marginals. Most importantly, the constants keep the directional geometry of the essential gradient ranges. For affine perturbations, our estimates recover the sharp noncommuting Caffarelli tensor. We further obtain pointwise displacement bounds for Gaussian perturbations and spectral Hessian estimates for compact Gaussian mixtures.

</details>


### [95] [Smooth autonomous fast dynamo action on the three-torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04153)
*Michele Coti Zelati,Massimo Sorella,David Villringer*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文构造了三维环面\mathbb{T}^3上一族非空的C^k-开集，其中包含光滑、自治、无散的快发电机速度场（fast dynamo），从而解决了Zeldovich和Sakharov提出的快发电机猜想（收录于Arnold的数学问题集中）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快发电机猜想是磁流体动力学中一个长期悬而未决的核心问题，涉及流场运动如何在耗散趋于零时仍能产生指数增长的磁场。此前仅有时间周期或近似的快发电机构造，尚不清楚是否存在光滑自治的无散速度场能在扩散系数\varepsilon\to0时保持均匀的磁增长速率。该问题的解决是动力系统与磁流体动力学交叉领域的重要突破。

Method: 论文采用两阶段构造策略：首先通过"拉伸-折叠-剪切"（stretch-fold-shear）机制建立一类光滑时间周期快发电机的族；随后利用全局Poincaré截面和适当构造的回归时间函数，将相应的周期动力学嵌入到一个光滑自治流中。在证明方面，先在分布的各向异性Banach空间中为理想感应算子（\varepsilon=0极限）分离出非稳定的分布性本征模，再证明该谱不稳定性在奇异摄动\varepsilon\Delta下持续存在。所有估计对\varepsilon\to0是均匀的，并且对速度场的C^k扰动稳定。

Result: 该方法对C^k小扰动具有稳定性，成功产生了一族在原空间意义下开的光滑快发电机矢量场，完成了快发电机猜想的正面解决。

Conclusion: 通过巧妙结合周期流的拉伸-折叠-剪切机制与自治化嵌入技术，以及基于各向异性分布空间的谱稳定性方法，本文在光滑自治无散流的最佳情形下解决了快发电机猜想，确立了开集中的快发电机存在性。

Abstract: We construct a nonempty $C^k$-open family, for some $k\in\mathbb{N}$, of smooth, autonomous, divergence-free velocity fields on $\mathbb{T}^3$ that generate fast dynamos. This resolves the Fast Dynamo Conjecture of Zeldovich and Sakharov, as recorded in Arnold's book of problems. We first construct a family of smooth, time-periodic fast dynamos via a stretch-fold-shear mechanism that applies to a large class of velocity fields. We then realize the associated period dynamics within a smooth autonomous flow using a global Poincaré section and a suitably prescribed return-time function. In both settings, the proof first isolates unstable distributional eigenmodes for the ideal induction operator on an anisotropic Banach space of distributions and then shows that this spectral instability persists under the singular perturbation $\varepsilon Δ$. The resulting estimates are uniform as $\varepsilon\to0$ and stable under $C^k$ perturbations of the velocity field, yielding an open set of smooth fast dynamo vector fields.

</details>

### [96] [The Geometry of Ignorance: LLMs Know When to Temper Bayesian Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02959)
*Toni J. B. Liu,Jiajun Bao,Yizhou Liu,Gurbir Arora,Nicolas Boullé,Raphaël Sarfati,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现语言模型的unembedding矩阵中存在一个单一方向编码了训练语料的unigram分布，作为模型在不确定时依赖的贝叶斯先验。将此方向投影可得到先验载荷因子λ，其随上下文信息增加而下降，并可通过温度化贝叶斯更新进行形式化解构，使λ可在不同模型规模和家族间有意义地比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型在信息不足时的预测行为机制——即模型的不确定性先验从何而来，以及如何在几何上量化和操控这种先验依赖。

Method: 在Llama、Qwen、Gemma和Pythia四种模型家族（0.4B至405B参数）中识别unembedding矩阵中编码unigram分布的"无知方向"（direction of ignorance）；通过将最终预测状态投影到该方向定义先验载荷因子λ；形式上将预测状态分解为两个正交向量，对应于温度化贝叶斯更新的两个因子（unigram先验和对数似然）；并通过干预实验验证该方向的因果作用。

Result: 该方向在四种模型家族中均存在；λ随上下文信息增加而稳定下降；分解结果精确对应于温度化贝叶斯更新公式；较大模型在高上下文情境中先验依赖通常更低；通过增减λ可在KL散度上引导预测朝向或远离unigram先验。

Conclusion: unembedding几何中编码的unigram先验构成了模型在缺乏线索时的认知基底，先验载荷因子λ提供了跨模型规模和家族可比较的量化指标，且该方向是因果可干预的，可用于可控地调节模型的先验依赖程度。

Abstract: What does a language model predict when it has few clues? The answer lurks in its unembedding geometry: a single direction of the unembedding matrix encodes the unigram distribution of the training corpus, which serves as the Bayesian prior the model falls back on when uncertain. This structure --- which we term the \emph{direction of ignorance} --- appears in all four model families examined (\texttt{Llama}, \texttt{Qwen}, \texttt{Gemma}, and \texttt{Pythia}), ranging from 0.4B to 405B parameters. Projecting the final prediction state onto this direction yields a per-token \emph{prior loading factor} $λ$, which, empirically, declines steadily as the context becomes more informative. Formally, the same projection decomposes the prediction state into two orthogonal vectors that correspond exactly to the two factors of a tempered Bayesian update: a unigram prior raised to the exponent $λ$ and a context-driven likelihood. This geometric-probabilistic interpretation calibrates $λ$, making it meaningfully comparable across model sizes and families, with larger models generally exhibiting lower prior reliance in the high-context limit. Finally, we show that the direction of ignorance is causally active: raising or lowering $λ$ at the final prediction state steers the prediction toward or away from the unigram prior in KL divergence.

</details>


### [97] [Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02987)
*Shrinivas Ramasubramanian,Daman Arora,Fahim Tajwar,Guanning Zeng,Qingyang Wu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng,Yuda Song,Aarti Singh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,J. Andrew Bagnell,Jeff Schneider,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tail-Likelihood强化学习（TailRL），不直接优化平均奖励，而是最大化策略在随机奖励阈值下超出该阈值的对数概率（即所有上尾概率的期望），从而重点关注稀有高奖励的rollout。该方法仅需修改优势函数即可与现有RL管线兼容，在物体定位、迷宫导航、GUI grounding和代码优化等任务上优于平均奖励优化方法，并能在推理时更好地利用额外采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习优化平均奖励，但对于生成式策略，两个策略可能在平均奖励相同的情况下，产生稀有高奖励rollout的概率差异很大。随着训练和推理时采样数量的增加，保留高奖励结果上的概率质量变得至关重要，平均奖励无法捕捉这一重要区别。因此需要一种直接优化覆盖稀有高奖励结果的优化目标。

Method: 将连续的奖励转化为一族二值成功事件（每个奖励阈值对应一个事件），提出TailRL目标：最大化两个随机量（阈值和策略rollout）联合下的对数超出概率。其梯度会为稀有高奖励rollout赋予更大权重，可解释为Best-of-(k)梯度的混合体。实现上只需对已有的优势函数做简单修改。

Result: 在物体定位、迷宫导航、GUI grounding和代码优化四个任务中，TailRL利用稀有高奖励训练样本避免陷入次优解，得到的模型在推理时能从额外的采样中获得更大的收益（相比平均奖励优化方法）。

Conclusion: 通过对奖励上尾（tail）概率的直接优化，TailRL为生成式策略更有效地保留稀有高奖励的概率质量，是一种与现有RL框架高度兼容且能显著提升采样效率的替代优化目标。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically optimizes average reward. For generative policies, the average can hide an important distinction: two policies can achieve the same mean reward while having very different chances of producing a rare but high-reward rollout. This matters as sampling increases during training and inference, since its benefit depends on retaining probability mass on high-reward outcomes. We propose to optimize this coverage directly. Rather than considering only expected reward, we consider all of its upper tails: for each reward threshold, how likely is the policy to exceed it? This turns a continuous reward into a family of binary success events. We introduce Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL), which maximizes the log-probability of exceeding a randomly chosen reward threshold. Its gradient gives more weight to rare, high-reward rollouts and can be interpreted as a mixture of Best-of-(k) gradients. TailRL requires only a simple modification to the advantage function, making it compatible with existing reinforcement learning pipelines. Across object localization, maze navigation, GUI grounding, and code optimization, TailRL leverages rare high-reward training samples to avoid suboptimal solutions and yields models that benefit more from additional samples at inference time.

</details>


### [98] [Equation Recast for Canonical Operator Learning Across Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02982)
*Qiyun Cheng,Valentin Duruisseaux,Cesar F. Clauser,Md Hossain Sahadath,Huihua Yang,Shaowu Pan,Nathaniel Ferraro,Anima Anandkumar,Wei Ji,Cristina Rea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"equation recast"（方程重构）框架，将参数化算子学习重构为学习单一规范算子，从控制方程中解析地推导参数引起的算子变化并将其吸收为有效源项，从而支持跨新参数域的零样本预测，在多参数、非线性、奇异PDE以及核聚变托卡马克仿真中验证了外推能力、异构数据整合及内部收敛告警机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的参数化算子模型需要大量覆盖输入函数和物理参数，且超出训练分布时可能无声失败（fail silently），缺乏可靠性与可泛化性。

Method: 提出方程重构（equation recast）：将参数化算子学习转化为单一规范算子的学习。参数诱导的算子变化通过控制方程解析推导，并被吸收为有效源项（effective sources），从而对不同参数域实现零样本预测。

Result: 在多参数、非线性和奇异PDE场景中支持跨参数外推；可通过共享规范表示整合稀疏异构数据集；利用重构迭代的收敛丧失作为内部告警信号。在核聚变高保真托卡马克仿真中，通过规范域映射统一了四种设备几何结构下的电子温度数据，并在一个联合训练的算子中实现统一预测。

Conclusion: 方程重构为可复用的神经PDE求解器提供了一条路径，融合了方程引导的迁移、数据效率和可监测的推理能力。

Abstract: Learning solution operators across broad parameter ranges can require substantial coverage of both input functions and physical parameters, particularly for purely data-driven parametric models. In addition, the resulting models may fail silently outside the training distribution. We introduce equation recast, which reformulates parametric operator learning as the learning of a single canonical operator. Parameter-induced operator variations are derived analytically from the governing equation and absorbed into effective sources, enabling zero-shot prediction across new parameter regimes. Across multi-parameter, nonlinear, and singular PDE settings, equation recast supports extrapolation, integrates sparse heterogeneous datasets in a shared canonical representation, and uses loss of convergence as an internal warning signal for failure of the recast iteration. In high-fidelity tokamak simulations for nuclear fusion, the framework unifies electron-temperature data across four device geometries through canonical-domain mapping within one jointly trained operator. Equation recast provides a route toward reusable neural PDE solvers combining equation-guided transfer, data efficiency, and monitorable inference.

</details>


### [99] [TRACE: Spatiotemporal Contact Memory Graph Network Simulator for Granular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02991)
*Changjian Zhou,Negin Yousefpour,Jie Qi,Junfeng Fang,Guillermo A. Narsilio,Hans Petter Jostad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE，一种将相互作用历史直接存储在接触边上的图网络模拟器，用于颗粒动力学长时程模拟。TRACE通过边缘持久记忆（注意力消息传递+GRU）和边缘身份字典来保持接触历史，并在2D和3D颗粒柱坍塌基准上表现优于现有方法，降低长程位置误差31-62%、最终沉积误差58-89%，同时比MPM参考求解器快8.9-12.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒运动强烈依赖于颗粒间的接触历史，而颗粒接触的形成、断裂和重排使得历史信息难以保存。现有图网络模拟器主要将时间信息存储在节点特征或节点级记忆上，无法充分捕捉接触历史的演化。

Method: TRACE在图网络的接触边上维护持久记忆。具体包括：1）每条接触边通过注意力机制的消息传递和门控循环单元（GRU）更新其持续记忆；2）边缘身份字典在接触图拓扑变化时保留相应记忆；3）物理结构的解码器预测颗粒间法向和切向接触力，强制执行库仑摩擦极限，并施加等大反向的内力；4）训练策略采用单步预训练后接自回归滚动微调。

Result: 在2D和3D颗粒柱坍塌基准上，TRACE产生稳定且物理一致的长时间滚动，准确再现最终沉积形态和坍塌释放的动能。与GNS和NMGNS相比，TRACE将长滚动位置误差降低31-62%，最终沉积误差降低58-89%，同时参数更少且颗粒间近似零穿透。相比MPM参考求解器，在2D和3D分别实现12.2倍和8.9倍加速。

Conclusion: 将相互作用历史存储在接触边缘（而非节点）上，能够更有效地捕捉颗粒接触历史的动态变化，从而显著提升图网络模拟器在颗粒动力学长时程预测中的准确性和稳定性，同时保持计算效率优势。

Abstract: Learned graph simulators provide an efficient alternative to high-fidelity solvers for granular dynamics. However, granular motion depends strongly on inter-granular contact history, which is difficult to preserve when particle contacts form, break, and rearrange. Existing simulators mainly store temporal information in node features or node-level memory. Here we introduce TRACE, a graph-network simulator that stores interaction history directly on contact edges. Each edge maintains a persistent memory updated by attention-based message passing and a gated recurrent unit, while an edge-identity dictionary preserves this memory as the contact graph changes. A physics-structured decoder predicts inter-granular normal and tangential contact forces, enforces the Coulomb friction limit, and applies equal-and-opposite internal forces. The model is trained with single-step pretraining followed by autoregressive rollout fine-tuning. We evaluate TRACE on 2D and 3D granular column-collapse benchmarks. In both cases, TRACE produces stable, physically consistent long-horizon rollouts, closely reproducing the final deposit geometry and the kinetic energy released during collapse. Compared with graph network simulator (GNS) and node-memory graph neural simulator (NMGNS), TRACE reduces long-rollout position error by 31-62% and final-deposit error by 58-89% across the two benchmarks, while using fewer parameters and maintaining near-zero particle interpenetration. TRACE also achieves 12.2$\times$ and 8.9$\times$ speedups over the material point method (MPM) reference solver in 2D and 3D, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/Data-Driven-Computational-Geotechnics/TRACE.

</details>


### [100] [From Euclidean to Graph-Structured Data: A Survey of Collaborative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02984)
*Rémi Bourgerie,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Viktoria Fodor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于协作学习（collaborative learning）从欧氏数据扩展到图结构数据的综合综述，系统梳理了联邦学习和去中心化学习在传统数据与图数据上的研究，提出了面向图数据的分布式场景分类法、统计异质性刻画以及标准化的问题定义与算法框架，并指出了开放挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习（集中式收集数据、训练和推理）受限于可扩展性和隐私问题。协作学习（联邦学习、去中心化学习）应运而生，但多数研究聚焦于欧氏数据（如图像、文本），忽略了众多真实应用中由关系模式构成的图数据。图学习依赖节点间消息传递机制，与协作环境中智能体交换信息的需求天然契合，但该方向的机会与挑战仍未被充分探索。

Method: 本文以综述方式展开：首先回顾协作学习在欧氏数据上的理论基础，并围绕学习有效性（effectiveness）、效率（efficiency）和隐私保护（privacy preservation）三个核心维度加以组织；随后将讨论拓展至图结构数据，引入图分布场景（graph distribution scenarios）的分类法，刻画相关统计异质性，并制定标准化的数学问题与算法框架；最后系统归纳开放挑战与有前景的研究方向。

Result: 该综述整合了从欧氏数据到图数据的协作学习研究，形成了该新兴领域的统一认知框架，包括三维度基础原则、图分布场景分类法、统计异质性描述以及标准化的问题定义和算法体系，并识别出尚待解决的开放性问题与潜在研究路径。

Conclusion: 协作学习与图结构数据的结合是一个尚处于早期但极具潜力的方向；通过本次系统综述，本文为该领域确立了初步的概念基础和统一的表述范式，为后续研究者提供了清晰的研究蓝图，同时明确指出仍需攻克的理论和实践难题（如同质与异质图分布下的学习、效率与隐私权衡等）。

Abstract: The conventional approach to machine learning, that is, collecting data, training models, and performing inference in a single location, faces fundamental limitations, including scalability and privacy, that restrict its applicability. To address these challenges, recent research has explored collaborative learning approaches, including federated learning and decentralized learning, where individual agents perform training and inference locally, with limited collaboration. Most collaborative learning research focuses on Euclidean data with regular, grid-like structure (e.g., images, text). However, these approaches fail to capture the relational patterns in many real-world applications, best represented by graphs. Learning on graphs relies on message-passing mechanisms to propagate information between connected nodes, making it conceptually well-suited for collaborative environments where agents must exchange information. Yet, the opportunities and challenges of learning on graph-structured data in collaborative settings remain largely underexplored. This survey provides a comprehensive investigation of collaborative learning from Euclidean to graph-structured data, aiming to consolidate this emerging field. We begin by reviewing its foundational principles for Euclidean data, organizing them along three core dimensions: learning effectiveness, efficiency, and privacy preservation. We then extend the discussion to graph-structured data, introducing a taxonomy of graph distribution scenarios, characterizing associated statistical heterogeneities, and developing standardized problem formulations and algorithmic frameworks. Finally, we systematically identify open challenges and promising research directions.

</details>


### [101] [No-Regret Bayesian Optimization with Finite-Library Input-Warped Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02993)
*Edvin Ketabati Augustinsson,Robert A. Bridges*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有限库输入扭曲贝叶斯优化（FLIWBO），通过从有限平滑映射库中自适应选择输入扭曲来适应几何失配，在保留高概率收敛保证（含显式√(N_ε)库大小代价）的同时提升样本效率。在合成目标、置信围栏陷阱、Fashion-MNIST超参数优化及20维多智能体系统设计等基准上，FLIWBO-UCB优于原始坐标GP-UCB并可规避传统陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-UCB等贝叶斯优化方法的收敛性证明依赖于固定核函数，但固定核编码的输入几何在坐标失配（如对数尺度超参数或局部峰）时效率低下。现有输入扭曲方法缺乏保留收敛保证的可行方案，如何在自适应调整输入几何的同时维持理论保证是核心挑战。

Method: FLIWBO从有限平滑输入映射库中，依据任意历史依赖规则选取扭曲。其理论贡献在于在温和假设下保留了高概率收敛保证，并给出了显式的√(N_ε)库规模代价（N_ε为库大小）。该方法允许在扭曲选择与采样过程中灵活权衡历史数据。

Result: 在多个基准上，FLIWBO-UCB：1）在几何失配下优于原始坐标GP-UCB；2）能逃离连oracle扭曲期望改进（EI）都无法逃脱的置信围栏陷阱；3）恢复了手动对数缩放的大部分收益；4）在匹配遗憾保证的方法中表现领先。20维MAS设计实验验证了在昂贵噪声评估下的可行性。

Conclusion: FLIWBO表明可以针对有限库输入扭曲在保留GP-UCB式收敛保证的同时实现自适应几何学习，为具备理论保证的可变形输入空间贝叶斯优化提供了新途径，并给出了明确的库规模-收敛权衡刻画。

Abstract: Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization (GP-BO) excels at black-box optimization of costly functions, e.g., hyperparameter optimization (HPO) and multi-agent system (MAS) design. Convergence-rate guarantees exist for select methods, notably GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB), but require a fixed kernel. Critically, the kernel encodes how input proximity affects objective value similarity. When raw coordinates poorly match this geometry - as with log-scaled hyperparameters or localized peaks - input warping can greatly improve sample efficiency, yet known GP-UCB proofs require a fixed kernel. We propose Finite-Library Input-Warped Bayesian Optimization (FLIWBO), which selects warps from a finite library of smooth input maps by any history-dependent rule. It adapts the input geometry to accelerate learning while retaining high-probability convergence guarantees under mild hypotheses, with an explicit $\sqrt(N_\varepsilon)$ library-size cost. Controlled diagnostics show that finite-library warping repairs planted geometry mismatches and identify FLIWBO failure cases. Across four repeated benchmarks - warped synthetic objectives, a confidence-fence trap, and Fashion-MNIST HPO - FLIWBO-UCB beats raw-coordinate GP-UCB under misspecified geometry, escapes traps that defeat even oracle-warp expected improvement, and recovers much of the gain from manual log scaling, while leading the tested methods that admit a matching regret guarantee. A 20-dimensional MAS design study further shows feasibility under costly noisy evaluations. Code for experiments is available: https://github.com/edvin-ketabati/bogp-paper-experiments.

</details>


### [102] [Modern Transformers Are Implicit Hybrids: From Functional Differentiation to Principled Hybrid Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02986)
*Runlin Shi,Bojian Yin,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RFIS与RPD两种干预指标，用以分析RoPE-based Transformer中头级的功能组织，发现检索头与位置头之间存在由训练长度位置尺度决定的Global Positional Band（GPBand）边界，并据此提出两条设计原则，实例化为Head-wise Hybrid Architecture（HwH），以极低的FA-to-LA比例（低于1:3）在提升检索与零样本长上下文外推的同时，保持语言建模与常识推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的FA与LA混合架构在分配上依赖启发式方法，缺乏证据基础。作者希望基于RoPE Transformer头级功能组织的证据，为混合架构设计提供可解释、可验证的原则，而非盲目组合。

Method: 提出两个干预性指标：RFIS（RoPE频率重要性分数，衡量每个频率对注意力分布的影响）与RPD（RoPE位置依赖性，隔离对旋转位置调制的依赖）。在Qwen3系列与Llama3.1上验证完整的两分类（检索头vs位置头）分类法；通过控制变量Transformer揭示该边界由训练长度位置尺度决定，命名为GPBand。据此提出两条原则（位置建模仅限局部、全局访问依赖位置无关检索；两者按头粒度分配且层级特异），并实例化HwH架构（NoPE FA负责全局检索，LA负责局部位置建模）。

Result: 在FA-to-LA比例低于1:3时，HwH在保持语言建模与常识推理能力的同时，改善了检索性能，并显著增强了相对Transformer、纯LA及层级混合基线模型的零样本长上下文外推能力；消融实验验证了两条设计原则及各组件的角色。

Conclusion: 头级功能（检索与位置）在GPBand处分离，这一组织规律为混合架构设计提供了证据基础；有原则的混合架构设计（按头分配、层级特异）是实现未来基础模型的可行且富有前景的路线。

Abstract: Hybrid architectures combining Full Attention (FA) and Linear Attention (LA) are increasingly prominent, yet their allocation remains heuristic. We seek an evidence-grounded basis in head-level functional organization learned by RoPE-based Transformers. Behavioral probes do not yield a complete taxonomy, so we propose two intervention metrics: RoPE Frequency Importance Score (RFIS), measuring how each frequency affects a head's attention distribution, and RoPE Positional Dependence (RPD), isolating dependence on rotary positional modulation. On Qwen3-series models and Llama3.1, RFIS suggests and RPD verifies a complete taxonomy of retrieval and positional heads separated by a salient mid-low-frequency band. Controlled Transformers show that this boundary follows the training-length positional scale; we term it the Global Positional Band (GPBand). The analysis suggests a potential cause of zero-shot length-extrapolation failure and yields two principles: positional modeling should operate only locally, with global access through position-independent retrieval; and both functions should be assigned at head granularity with layer-specific allocation. We instantiate them in Head-wise Hybrid Architecture (HwH), using NoPE FA for global retrieval and LA for local positional modeling. With an FA-to-LA ratio below 1:3, HwH retains strong language modeling and commonsense reasoning while improving retrieval and substantially strengthening zero-shot long-context extrapolation over Transformer, LA, and a layer-wise hybrid baseline. Ablations validate both principles and component roles, highlighting principled hybrid architecture design as a promising route toward future foundation models.

</details>


### [103] [Evaluating Graph Neural Networks for Change-Criticality Classification in Maritime Navigation Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02996)
*Abhishek Potnis,Jacob Arndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将电子航海图（ENC）中的空间矢量数据表示为图结构，其中空间对象作为节点、空间与语义关系作为边，将ENC新旧数据集的变化检测建模为图对分类问题，并评估多种图神经网络（GNN）架构以区分对航行安全构成关键或非关键风险的更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子航海图（ENC）数据维护是一项挑战，对ENC中对象变化按航行安全重要性进行分类尤为关键。目前尚不清楚哪种消息传递操作、架构配置和图表示最适合该分类任务，因此需要探索基于图的表示与学习方法来自动化此过程。

Method: 将两个版本的ENC矢量导航数据集（旧版与新版）分别编码为一对图结构，空间对象作为节点，空间与语义关系构成边；将任务定义为图对分类问题；基于此表示训练并评估多种GNN架构与模型配置，分类结果标记为对航行安全的关键或非关键风险。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于图的表示能够有效提升ENC更新的分类性能，提供了一种可扩展的方法来自动化或改进ENC维护工作流程。

Conclusion: 图神经网络结合图对分类框架可以有效地应用于电子航海图的空间数据变化分类任务，相比传统方法具有更好的性能和可扩展性，有望成为ENC维护流程中可靠的自动化辅助工具。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are a class of neural networks suitable for learning on graph-structured data. Their application to spatial data is a natural extension, however its relatively unclear which message-passing operations, architectural configurations, and graph representation is best suited for classifying changes to objects in electronic navigational charts (ENCs)--geospatial vector datasets used for marine navigation. Maintaining these datasets is a challenge, and categorizing changes to objects in the ENC based on their significance to navigational safety is of particular importance. Here, we propose to represent these vector navigation datasets as a graph structure where the spatial objects serve as nodes and their spatial and semantic relationships form edges. We encode both the old ENC dataset and new ENC dataset into a pair of graphs and frame the task as a graph-pair classification problem. Building on this representation, we investigate the use of GNN architectures to classify whether the encoded graphs constitutes a critical or non-critical risk to navigational safety. We train and evaluate several GNN architectures and model configurations on ENC changes reviewed by maritime experts. Our results demonstrate that graph-based representations improve the classification of ENC updates, providing a scalable approach for automating or improving ENC maintenance workflows.

</details>


### [104] [Causal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03003)
*Christopher Stith,Hossein Rahmani,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了因果基础模型（Causal Foundation Models, CFMs）这一新兴领域——将基础模型的范式引入因果推断，即在大规模数据上预训练神经网络，使其能在全新数据集上通过上下文学习直接估计因果量（如平均处理效应），无需微调或更新模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断需要针对每个新问题定制专属的计算流程：先提出因果机制、再选择合适的估计器、最后进行训练，效率低下且缺乏通用性。与此同时，机器学习领域已广泛转向基础模型范式（一次性大规模预训练、无需微调即可应用于新任务），因果推断需要拥抱这一范式以提升通用性和实用性。

Method: 本文是一篇实践性入门综述。首先系统梳理因果推断和机器学习所需的背景知识，然后介绍因果基础模型（CFMs）——即在大规模预训练的神经网络，能够在全新数据集上通过上下文学习（in-context learning）估计处理效应等因果量，无需任何模型更新。文中包含示例代码和Jupyter notebooks作为教学辅助。

Result: 本文为读者提供了CFMs领域的实践性入门指南，总结了相关背景知识，阐释了CFMs如何将基础模型范式应用于因果估计，并配备了可运行的示例代码和Jupyter notebooks帮助读者理解与实践。

Conclusion: 因果基础模型代表了因果推断向基础模型范式转变的重要方向，有望使因果估计像现代机器学习中的其他任务一样实现"预训练一次、通用应用"，降低因果推断的应用门槛并提升跨场景的迁移能力。

Abstract: Causal inference is the practice of estimating the effect of a treatment or intervention from data. It traditionally requires a bespoke pipeline for every new problem: first proposing a causal mechanism, selecting a compatible estimator, and finally training it. Meanwhile, across diverse settings and modalities, much of machine learning has shifted to the paradigm of foundation models: networks pretrained once at scale and applied to new tasks without fine-tuning. Causal foundation models (CFMs) bring this paradigm to causal inference. CFMs are pretrained neural networks that estimate causal quantities, such as the average treatment effect, on entirely new datasets using in-context learning without requiring model updates. This work provides a practical introduction to this emerging area. We summarize the necessary background in causal inference and machine learning before discussing CFMs. Throughout, we include example code and Jupyter notebooks.

</details>


### [105] [Mesh-Native Physics-Informed Graph Surrogates for TCAD-in-the-Loop Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02988)
*Leonid Popryho,Ayoub Sadeghi,Inna Partin-Vaisband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种物理信息图注意力网络（GAT）替代模型，直接在四面体TCAD网格上预测漂移-扩散方程组的基本未知量（静电势、电子和空穴准费米能级），实现跨器件几何尺寸的泛化，并通过深度集成主动学习加速多目标设计空间探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真TCAD仿真计算开销极大，尤其在3D结构下随网格复杂度陡增，严重限制多目标设计空间探索。现有机器学习替代模型只能将固定设计参数映射为少数标量器件指标，丢弃底层物理规律，且无法跨器件几何结构或器件族迁移。

Method: 提出物理信息图注意力网络（GAT）替代模型：直接在四面体TCAD网格上以图形式操作，预测每个网格节点上的静电势及电子、空穴准费米能级（漂移-扩散系统的三个基本未知场）。训练时结合数据损失与有限体积电流连续性残差，将载流子输运物理嵌入目标函数。由于在网格上以图方式操作，模型天然具备尺寸泛化能力（少鳍训练可适用大阵列）。使用深度集成的逐节点不确定性驱动主动学习循环，快速筛选候选设计。

Result: 在Sentaurus Device基准测试的多鳍三栅FinFET上，替代模型对三个漂移-扩散场逐器件RMSE低于每场伏特级单位，单设计吞吐量比完整仿真器高数个数量级。优势随器件尺寸增大而扩大：在大规模多鳍阵列（直接仿真极慢）上推断仍可在每器件1秒内完成，实现直接TCAD扫描不可行的跨器件尺度Pareto前沿探索。

Conclusion: 物理信息GAT替代模型成功将机器学习代仿真扩展到完整器件物理场预测层面，兼具物理一致性、跨几何尺寸泛化性和极快推理速度，为大规模多目标FinFET设计空间探索提供了可行路径。

Abstract: High-fidelity TCAD simulation of drift-diffusion transport remains the workhorse of emerging FinFET device design, but it is computationally expensive, especially for 3D structures where runtime escalates steeply with mesh complexity. This sharply limits multi-objective design space exploration. Existing machine-learning surrogates map a fixed set of design parameters to a few scalar device metrics, discarding the underlying physics and losing transferability across device geometries and families. A physics-informed graph attention network (GAT) surrogate is proposed. It operates directly on the tetrahedral TCAD mesh and predicts, at every mesh node, the electrostatic potential together with the electron and hole quasi-Fermi levels, the fundamental unknowns of the drift-diffusion system. Training combines a data loss with finite-volume current-continuity residuals, embedding carrier-transport physics into the objective. Operating on the mesh as a graph, the surrogate inherits size generalization: a model trained on few-fin meshes applies unchanged to substantially larger arrays, bounded at inference only by GPU memory. Per-node uncertainty from a deep ensemble drives an active-learning loop that screens large candidate pools in seconds and forwards only the most informative designs for full simulation. Benchmarked against Sentaurus Device on multi-fin tri-gate FinFETs, the surrogate reproduces the three drift-diffusion fields with sub-volt per-field RMSE and reaches a per-design throughput orders of magnitude higher than the full simulator. The advantage grows with device size: on large multi-fin arrays that are prohibitively slow to simulate directly, inference still completes in under a second per device, enabling Pareto-front exploration across device scales infeasible for direct TCAD sweeps.

</details>


### [106] [Spectral characteristics of autoencoder parameters as a vector representation of data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03495)
*Maria Nikitina,Anton Bishuk,Oleg Bakhteev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了自编码器模型参数与训练数据统计特性之间的关系，提出可将自编码器模型参数视为训练样本的稠密向量表示。通过理论分析和实验验证，基于模型参数矩阵的奇异值所构建的向量表示能够高精度区分不同数据子集上训练得到的模型，为模型参数作为样本表征提供了理论与实验依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自编码器模型参数与训练数据统计特性之间的内在联系，旨在验证模型参数是否可以作为训练样本的向量表示，从而在不使用原始样本或复杂向量生成算法的情况下实现样本区分与表征。

Method: 首先提出假设：自编码器模型参数可被视为对应样本的稠密向量表示。然后进行理论分析，推导模型参数矩阵的奇异值与训练数据协方差矩阵特征值之间的关系，证明数据空间与参数空间之间的信息传递。最后在CIFAR-10和FashionMNIST数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 理论分析表明模型参数矩阵的奇异值与训练数据协方差矩阵的特征值存在数学关联；在CIFAR-10和FashionMNIST数据集上的实验证明，基于参数矩阵谱特征构建的向量表示能够以高精度区分在不同数据子集上训练的自编码器模型。

Conclusion: 训练后的自编码器参数可以被视为样本的表示形式，无需依赖原始样本或复杂的向量生成算法即可实现高效的模型间区分，这为模型参数空间与数据空间之间的信息传递提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: This paper examines the relationship between the parameters of autoencoder models and the statistical properties of the data on which they are trained. Autoencoders are defined as models with an encoder-decoder architecture, trained to reconstruct input data through a compressed latent representation. It is proposed that the model parameters can be viewed as a dense vector representation of the corresponding sample. To test this hypothesis, a theoretical and experimental study is conducted in which a vector representation is formed based on the spectral characteristics of the autoencoder parameter matrices. Theoretical analysis shows that the singular values of the model parameter matrices are related to the eigenvalues of the covariance matrix of the training data, ensuring the transfer of information between the data space and the parameter space. Experimental results on the CIFAR-10 and FashionMNIST datasets confirm that the resulting vector representations allow for a high degree of accuracy in distinguishing between models trained on different data subsets, without resorting to complex vector generation algorithms or using the original samples. These results suggest that the parameters of trained autoencoders can be viewed as sample representations.

</details>


### [107] [High-Dimensional Learning Dynamics of Attention-Indexed Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03858)
*Yizhou Xu,Margarita Sagitova,Lenka Zdeborová,Florent Krzakala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了注意力机制（attention）的训练动力学，提出了注意力索引模型（attention-indexed models）这一统一框架，揭示了注意力参数化本身可作为架构隐式偏置（implicit bias）影响学习动态，并通过动力学分析区分直连优化、绑定注意力（tied attention）与非绑定注意力（untied attention）在弱恢复阶段的本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制是现代基础模型的核心组件，但其训练动力学（尤其是当注意力矩阵具有高秩时）仍缺乏深入理解。理解不同注意力参数化（直接优化、绑定、非绑定）如何影响学习动态，对于设计高效的架构和训练策略具有重要意义。

Method: 提出attention-indexed models作为涵盖多层、多头注意力的泛化框架。在高维极限下，证明群体损失景观（population-loss landscape）可由有限维trace序参数（trace order parameters）刻画；而在线SGD则由无限层次矩阵矩（matrix moments）支配，但可被有限截断系统指数级近似。进而对不同参数化形式（直接优化S、绑定注意力S=WW^T、非绑定注意力S=UV^T）分别分析其训练动力学。

Result: 发现注意力参数化扮演架构隐式偏置角色：直接优化S可能陷入无信息状态；绑定注意力诱导自动对称破缺机制，在Θ(d²log d)样本量下实现弱恢复；非绑定注意力存在快慢机制，预激活均值先快速演化而重叠量slowly演化，当快动力学选择的态打破初始对称时，在Θ(d²log d)量级实现弱恢复。

Conclusion: 注意力参数化本身是决定学习动态与样本效率的关键架构因素，不同参数化在对称破缺机制上存在本质区别，这一发现为理解和设计具有良好训练特性的注意力架构提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern foundation models, yet their training dynamics remain poorly understood, especially when the attention matrices have extensive rank. In this work, we study attention-indexed models, a broad framework that can represent multi-layer and multi-head attention architectures. First, we show that, in a suitable high-dimensional limit, the population-loss landscape is characterized by a finite set of trace order parameters. In contrast, online stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is governed by an infinite hierarchy of matrix moments, which we show can be exponentially well-approximated by a finite truncated system. Second, this framework reveals that attention parameterization itself can act as an architectural implicit bias. Direct optimization of an attention matrix $S\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}$ can remain trapped in an uninformative state. Tied attention ($S=WW^\top$) induces an automatic symmetry-breaking mechanism and yields weak recovery in $Θ(d^2\log d)$ samples. For untied attention, $S=UV^\top$, we uncover a fast-slow mechanism: the pre-activation mean first evolves on a fast timescale, while the overlaps evolve on a slower one. Weak recovery on the $Θ(d^2\log d)$ scale occurs when the state selected by the fast dynamics breaks the initial symmetry.

</details>


### [108] [Verify Before You Distill: Prompt-Level Teacher Gating for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02998)
*Zhiwei Zhang,Zechen Sun,Fei Zhao,Kang Peng,Bin Liang,Huayu Deng,Yao Hu,Kam-Fai Wong,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation (TGOPD)方法：在逐prompt层面验证教师模型的可靠性，通过少量verifier评分教师探针来判断是否采用密集的on-policy蒸馏，不可靠时切换到verifier-grounded GRPO。在4B和35B规模的数学、代码和指令跟随任务上全面超越Vanilla OPD，并将异步OPD中教师节点GPU利用率从9.8%提升到78.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vanilla OPD对prompt均匀施加密集token级监督，未检验教师是否在每个prompt上可靠。由于reverse KL是mode-seeking的，当教师模型自信但错误时，会产生强而误导的更新。现有分布代理（如熵或师生似然一致性）只衡量不确定性或一致性，无法直接验证结果正确性。

Method: TGOPD的核心思想是在密集监督引入前，在prompt层面验证教师的可靠性。具体做法是：(1) 用少量verifier评分的教师probes来估计每个prompt上的教师可靠性；(2) 当可靠性检查通过时，将prompt专门路由到密集OPD；(3) 否则路由到verifier-grounded GRPO。该方法还利用了异步OPD中原本闲置的教师算力来进行可靠性估计。

Result: 在4B和35B学生模型上，覆盖数学、代码和指令跟随三类任务：TGOPD在所有六个单领域设置中均优于Vanilla OPD；在多领域训练下，两个规模上均获得更高的七个基准平均值。在教师侧，通过复用闲置算力进行可靠性估计，教师节点GPU利用率从9.8%提升至78.9%（4B单领域运行的实测值）。

Conclusion: 逐prompt验证教师可靠性是OPD中避免误导性更新、提升蒸馏效果的关键；TGOPD通过可靠性门控路由机制在性能上全面优于Vanilla OPD，同时提升了异步蒸馏系统的教师侧算力利用效率。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) accelerates post-training by providing dense token-level supervision from a frozen teacher on the student's own rollouts. Vanilla OPD applies this supervision uniformly across prompts, without checking whether the teacher is reliable for each prompt. Because reverse KL is mode-seeking, a confidently wrong teacher can induce a strong yet misleading update. Distributional proxies, such as entropy or teacher-student likelihood agreement, measure uncertainty or agreement but do not directly verify outcome correctness. We introduce Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation (TGOPD), built on the principle that teacher reliability should be verified at the prompt level before dense supervision is admitted. TGOPD estimates reliability from a small set of verifier-scored teacher probes and routes each prompt exclusively to dense OPD when the reliability check passes or to verifier-grounded GRPO otherwise. Across 4B and 35B students in mathematics, code, and instruction following, TGOPD outperforms Vanilla OPD in all six single-domain settings and achieves higher seven-benchmark averages at both scales under multi-domain training. By using otherwise-idle teacher capacity for reliability estimation, TGOPD also reduces teacher-side compute waste in asynchronous OPD, increasing teacher-node GPU utilization from 9.8% to 78.9% in the measured 4B single-domain run.

</details>


### [109] [ObserverBench: Testing Mechanistic Estimates for Intervention and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03026)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ObserverBench，一个用于评估机械可解释性方法在指导实际干预/控制/安全任务中是否真正有效的基准框架。其核心洞见是：内部估计器（观察者）即使在平均意义上准确，也可能在选择具体行动时表现不佳。该框架将估计精度与行动带来的损失分开报告，并通过理论和实验证明两者缺一不可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性越来越多地被用于指导激活引导、电路移除和安全监控等干预措施，但现有评估通常只关注平均估计精度而忽略了估计器在具体任务决策中的适用性。一个在平均意义上准确的内部估计仍然可能导致糟糕的行动选择，因此需要一个新的评估视角来检验内部估计器是否真正适合其所指导的干预、控制或安全任务。

Method: ObserverBench为每项任务固定模型、信息边界、允许的行动、决策规则、留出案例和损失函数，同时分别报告估计精度和由所选行动导致的损失。理论上分析了闭环控制中观察者误差在起始点及干预可达方向上的影响；实验涵盖GPT-2-small和Qwen2.5-7B上的电路干预任务、多个模型上的安全分流任务，对比成对观察者、基于行动损失训练的观察者及多层匹配的稠密与稀疏SAE读数。

Result: 实验表明：1）在电路干预任务中，成对观察者预测未见效果更准确但未必选择更好的行动，而基于行动损失训练的观察者能选择更低损失的行动；2）在安全分流中，完美分离违规的分数在违规成本不同时可能分配干预预算不佳；3）AUROC排序与部署损失的排序在不同模型上可能不一致（Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma-2-9B-it、冻结的Qwen3.5-9B APPS任务）；4）稀疏SAE读数在披露的激活密度或检查点不匹配下落后于层匹配的稠密对照。

Conclusion: 估计精度与行动导向的损失必须分开评估，两者不可替代。ObserverBench提供了固定的任务契约、可运行的基线和基于表格的提交方式，通过可解释性方法所启用的实际行动来评估其有效性，为可解释性方法走向实际干预应用提供了更严格和任务对齐的评估标准。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability is increasingly used to guide interventions such as activation steering, circuit removal, and safety monitoring. Yet an internal estimate that is accurate on average can still choose a poor action.
  We present ObserverBench, a benchmark framework for testing whether an internal estimator---an observer---is adequate for the intervention, control, or safety task it directs. Each task fixes the model, information boundary, allowed actions, decision rule, held-out cases, and loss. The benchmark reports estimation accuracy separately from the loss caused by the chosen action.
  Theory and experiments show why both are needed. In closed-loop control, observer errors matter at the starting point and along directions the allowed intervention can reach. On circuit-intervention tasks in GPT-2-small and Qwen2.5-7B, pairwise observers predict unseen effects more accurately without always choosing better actions; observers trained on action loss choose lower-loss actions. In safety triage, a score that perfectly separates violations can allocate a fixed intervention budget poorly when violations have different costs. Across Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma-2-9B-it, and prospectively frozen Qwen3.5-9B APPS tasks, AUROC can rank monitors differently from deployment loss, and the best information source changes across models. Sparse SAE readouts also trail their layer-matched dense controls on the reported Qwen panels, under disclosed activation-density or checkpoint mismatches.
  ObserverBench provides fixed task contracts, runnable baselines, and table-based submissions for evaluating interpretability methods through the actions they enable.

</details>


### [110] [Learnable composition for neural operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03069)
*Zituo Chen,Baiming Zhang,Sili Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LatentDDM提出一种"局部预训练+组合迁移"的神经算子设计范式：先在小子域上预训练神经算子预测物理场，部署到新场景时冻结该算子，仅训练一个轻量级模块来组合局部预测。在稳态Darcy流和大攻角俯仰翼型非定常流动两个问题上，相比容量匹配的全域模型，LatentDDM在适应后误差降低36-56%，并在零样本和少样本设置下均提升了多步rollout精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子作为物理仿真的快速可微替代模型，在域几何、尺寸或运行条件偏离训练数据时精度会显著退化。虽然有监督适应可以恢复精度，但即使使用很小的目标数据集，仍需昂贵的高保真仿真来生成标注。因此核心问题是：如何协同设计预训练与迁移策略，以降低部署时的数据采集成本。

Method: LatentDDM采用两步设计：(1) 在小尺寸子域上预训练神经算子（local pretraining）以预测物理场；(2) 面对新设定时，冻结预训练算子，仅训练一个轻量级模块将各子域的局部预测组合/拼接成全局解（composition-level transfer）。该方法在Darcy流问题中处理长程压力耦合随域扩大的挑战，在翼型绕流问题中应对目标俯仰频率超出训练范围导致的rollout误差累积问题。

Result: 在两类问题上验证：(1) 稳态Darcy流，在更大的多孔介质域上，使用16个目标仿真进行适应后，LatentDDM相比容量匹配的全域一次性处理模型，误差降低36-56%；(2) 非定常不可压缩翼型流（俯仰运动），在快俯仰工况下，LatentDDM在20步场rollout中无论是零样本还是少样本校准后均优于基线。

Conclusion: 该研究将"局部预训练+组合级迁移"识别为物理基础模型的一种有前景的设计原则，表明预训练策略（局部化）与迁移策略（轻量组合模块）的协同设计能显著降低神经算子部署到新场景所需的昂贵仿真数据量。

Abstract: Neural operators are fast, differentiable surrogates for physical simulation, but their accuracy often degrades when domain geometry, size, or operating conditions differ from training. Supervised adaptation can recover accuracy, but even a small target set requires costly high-fidelity simulations. We therefore ask how pretraining and transfer can be designed together to reduce this deployment cost. LatentDDM first pretrains a neural operator to predict fields on small subdomains. For a new setting, it freezes this operator and trains only a lightweight module that composes the local predictions. We evaluate our method on two complementary problems: steady Darcy flow, where long-range pressure coupling must extend across increasingly large porous domains, and unsteady incompressible flow around a pitching airfoil, where rollout errors compound as target pitching frequencies exceed the training range. Compared with the capacity-matched models that process the full domain at once, LatentDDM's error is 36-56% lower on larger Darcy domains after adaptation with 16 target simulations. It also improves 20-step field rollouts in fast-pitching airfoil flow, both zero-shot and after few-shot calibration. These results identify the co-designed local pretraining and composition-level transfer as a promising design principle for physical foundation models.

</details>


### [111] [LeanStream: A Speculate-and-Refine Streaming Framework for Efficient on-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03079)
*Renyuan Liu,Yuyang Leng,Kaiyan Liu,Yuzhou Zhong,Shaohan Hu,Chun-Fu,Chen,Peijun Zhao,Heechul Yun,Shuochao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeanStream是一个用于端侧LLM推理的流式推测-修正框架，通过利用部分GPU结果渐进式优化计算、加载与缓存保留决策，在移动和嵌入式平台上实现高效的GPU执行与存储I/O重叠。相比先前系统，在达到相同吞吐时内存使用降低4.8×~7.5×，同时吞吐提升1.6×~2.1×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧LLM推理受限于DRAM容量，模型权重远超可用内存。此前系统利用激活稀疏性并将权重卸载至SSD/闪存，但面临根本性系统权衡：精确的稀疏执行决策需要最新的上下文，而高效的计算-I/O重叠需要提前预测，导致已有设计要么串行执行，要么引入冗余权重读取、额外计算和较大缓存开销。

Method: LeanStream提出流式推测-修正（speculate-and-refine）框架，利用部分GPU计算结果逐渐细化（refine）计算优先级、权重加载优先级和缓存保留优先级，从而实现GPU执行与存储I/O之间的细粒度重叠，在权衡中同时兼顾决策准确性与调度效率。

Result: 在移动端和嵌入式平台实现，与先前端侧LLM推理系统相比，在先前工作达到的最佳吞吐水平下内存使用减少4.8×~7.5×，同时token生成吞吐进一步提高1.6×~2.1×。

Conclusion: LeanStream通过渐进式修正调度决策解决了端侧LLM推理中"决策准确性"与"调度效率"难以兼得的系统性问题，在降低内存开销的同时显著提升生成吞吐，为端侧大模型高效推理提供了有效范式。

Abstract: On-device LLM inference is attractive for privacy and responsiveness, but remains challenging on mobile and embedded devices because model weights far exceed available DRAM. Prior systems exploit activation sparsity and offload weights to SSD or flash storage, but face a fundamental systems trade-off: accurate sparse execution decisions require the latest context, whereas efficient computation-I/O overlap requires early prediction. As a result, existing designs either serialize execution or incur redundant weight fetches, extra computation, and large cache overheads. We present LeanStream, a streaming speculate-and-refine framework for efficient on-device LLM inference. LeanStream progressively refines computation, loading, and cache-retention priorities using partial GPU results, enabling fine-grained overlap between GPU execution and storage I/O. We implement LeanStream on both mobile and embedded platforms. Compared with prior on-device LLM inference systems, LeanStream reduces memory usage by 4.8$\times$ to 7.5$\times$ at the best throughput achieved by prior work, while further improving token generation throughput by 1.6$\times$ to 2.1$\times$.

</details>


### [112] [The Gradient Does Not See Rank: Rank-Indifference in Matrix-CODI on ProsQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03090)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了矩阵链式思维（matrix-CODI）模型中潜在矩阵Z的秩是否与推理路径数量相关。通过秩k投影消融实验发现，无论采用何种训练体制、读出头设计或数据规模，秩k准确率曲线均平坦（波动≤0.6个百分点），表明矩阵潜在表征不编码可通过秩捕捉的多分量推理结构。负对照实验显示，纯GPT-2 SFT模型在相同干预范式下同样呈现平坦曲线，说明秩消融本身无法区分"秩盲"与"位置无关性"两种假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续链式思维模型将推理压缩到潜在token中，而矩阵变体通过d×d矩阵瓶颈路由每个潜在token，使秩成为潜在矩阵Z上单样本的结构性可观测量。若矩阵潜在表征通过叠加承载并行推理路径，秩应能追踪这些路径数量，而将Z截断为低秩应损害需要多组件解决方案的任务精度。作者旨在检验该假设是否成立。

Method: 在矩阵-CODI模型的四种训练体制上（三个ProsQA变体，一个低于学习阈值的GSM8K-Aug）进行秩k投影消融；随后通过三种子重复实验验证。为排除"展平后投影读数"造成的秩盲，设计了四种新型读出头：双线性重参数化、双线性+GELU非线性读出、经MLP馈送奇异值的SVD增强读出、以及Z Z^T的二次读出。最后执行线性探针和GPT-2 SFT负对照实验。

Result: 秩k投影消融曲线在所有训练体制下平坦至0.6个百分点以内。三种子重复获得81.0±2.0个百分点准确率，而Z的有效最终秩跨{4，12,13}。四种读出头（双线性、双线性+GELU、SVD增强、二次）均维持平坦秩k曲线（Spearman p值分别为0.63、0.14、0.82、0.46）。线性探针在Z上表现逊于原始预训练隐藏状态（AUC 0.673 vs 0.846）。GPT-2 SFT负对照再现平坦曲线（合并均值范围0.20pp），随机h灵敏度底线落于相同准确率。

Conclusion: 矩阵潜在表征的秩并不编码样本的推理深度或并行路径数量；损失的训练动力学不奖励任何特定秩结构。秩k消融单独无法作为验证矩阵潜在表征中分量推理的证据——该干预与位置随机化（position-irrelevance）在因果效应上不可区分。平坦曲线的持续存在（即使在非线性读出头下）进一步表明秩盲性根植于潜在表征结构本身，而非读数架构。

Abstract: Continuous chain-of-thought models compress reasoning into latent tokens. Matrix-valued variants, which route each latent token through a d x d matrix bottleneck, introduce rank as a single-sample structural observable on the latent matrix Z. If matrix latents carry parallel reasoning paths via superposition, rank should track them, and truncating Z to low rank should hurt accuracy on tasks whose solutions plausibly require multiple components. Across four training regimes of a matrix-CODI model (three on ProsQA, one on GSM8K-Aug below the learning threshold), the rank-k projection ablation curve is flat to within 0.6 percentage points. A three-seed replication yields 81.0 +/- 2.0 percentage points accuracy while the final effective rank of Z spans {4, 12, 13}; the loss does not reward any particular rank. To test whether rank-blindness arises from the flatten-then-project readout alone, we trained four readouts: a bilinear reparametrization, a bilinear-plus-GELU readout nonlinear in Z, an SVD-augmented readout feeding singular values through an MLP, and a quadratic readout in Z Z^T. All four rank-k curves remain flat (Spearman p-values 0.63, 0.14, 0.82, 0.46). The flat curves persist for readouts nonlinear in Z. A linear probe on Z underperforms a raw pretrained hidden state at target prediction (AUC 0.673 vs. 0.846). A negative control on vanilla GPT-2 SFT (no matrix bottleneck, no Z, three seeds, n=500) reproduces a flat rank-k curve under the same intervention paradigm with pooled-mean range 0.20pp, and a random-h sensitivity floor lands at the same accuracy: the rank-k ablation alone conflates rank-blindness with position-irrelevance.

</details>


### [113] [Distilling deep optical flow stereo methods to retrieve dense three-dimensional wind fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03100)
*Thomas J. Vandal,Dong L. Wu,James L. Carr,Derek J. Posselt,Elise Penn,Tristan Ballard,August Posch,Kate Duffy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用深度光流替代传统的基于窗口互相关的立体匹配方法，实现静止轨道大气运动矢量（AMV）的高效、高精度反演，并通过知识蒸馏将多卫星立体教师模型压缩为单卫星学生模型，在消除多重覆盖需求的同时保持精度与不确定性估计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AMV反演存在三个问题：一是基于红外亮温配合NWP背景估计高度，形成循环依赖导致高度不准；二是基于窗口的交叉相关跟踪计算成本高、检索稀疏；三是立体风方法虽几何上消解高度、不依赖NWP，但计算量大且覆盖有限。

Method: 用深度光流替换立体匹配中的窗口跟踪；通过微调平衡自监督几何残差损失与有监督探空仪重建损失；利用知识蒸馏将多卫星立体教师模型蒸馏为单卫星学生模型，并让学生模型模拟教师的卡方统计量与高度不确定性以实现质量控制。

Result: 三重一致性验证（triple collocation）结果表明，立体风在水汽波段（6.2、6.9和7.3μm）的性能优于运行AMV，而在长波红外波段（11.2μm）存在退化。

Conclusion: 深度光流结合知识蒸馏方案能够显著提升立体风反演效率并消除多卫星重叠依赖，生成全球全圆盘GEO影像的风场，在水汽波段上超越现有操作AMV，为AMV反演提供了一种高效可行的替代范式。

Abstract: Geostationary atmospheric motion vectors (AMVs) provide the dense horizontal wind vectors (u,v) and heights ingested into data assimilation systems. Traditional AMVs track features using window-based cross-correlation and estimate heights via infrared brightness temperatures paired with numerical weather prediction (NWP) background states, creating a circular dependency that yields inaccurate heights, high computational cost, and sparse retrievals. Stereo winds from GEO-GEO and GEO-LEO geometrically resolve heights from parallax shifts across different poses, eliminating NWP dependence and improving accuracy, but they remain computationally heavy with limited coverage. In this work, we replace window-based tracking in stereo matching with deep optical flow for efficient, improved retrieval. Fine-tuning balances a self-supervised geometric residual loss with supervised radiosonde reconstruction. To eliminate multi-satellite overlap requirements, we distill the stereo teacher into a single-satellite student model. Chi-square and height uncertainties from the teacher are emulated by the student for quality assurance. The student generates winds across full-disk GEO imagery globally. Validation compares stereo and student models against radiosondes, operational AMVs, ERA5 reanalysis, and EarthCARE cloud profiles. Results through triple collocation show that stereo winds improve performance beyond operational AMVs for water vapor bands (6.2, 6.9, and 7.3 μm), wit degradation in the long-wave infrared (11.2 μm) band.

</details>


### [114] [Scaling Laws, Tabular Data and Actuarial Ratemaking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03106)
*Ronald Richman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了深度学习中的缩放定律（scaling laws）是否适用于精算费率厘定（actuarial ratemaking）任务。通过在一个真实机动车保险组合上训练多种模型族（TabM、Transformer、MLP等），评估不同训练数据比例下的泊松偏差（Poisson deviance），发现所有模型族都随数据增加而改善，但缩放指数差异显著：TabM的数据缩放能力远强于监督式表格Transformer和标准MLP。Transformer变体除非引入额外归纳偏置（TabM式适配或自监督），否则参数缩放表现较弱。结论是精算表格任务的有效缩放取决于架构和损失函数目标设计，单纯增大Transformer规模收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习领域已广泛验证缩放定律（随模型容量、数据和计算量增长，损失呈幂律下降），但精算费率厘定场景中的数据是表格型、异质且高噪声的，经典模型（如GLM）仍然表现强劲。作者希望探究在此类实际领域中是否也存在类似的缩放规律，从而为模型选择提供数据规模维度的定量指导。

Method: 使用真实机动车保险组合数据，对多个模型族（TabM、监督式表格Transformer、标准MLP、Transformer变体等）在不同训练数据比例和多个随机种子下进行训练，以样本外泊松偏差（likelihood-based loss for Poisson count predictions）作为评估指标，比较各模型的缩放行为。

Result: 所有模型族均随数据增加而表现提升，但数据缩放指数差异显著：TabM的数据缩放远强于监督式表格Transformer和MLP基线。Transformer变体在缺少额外归纳偏置时参数缩放表现弱；引入TabM式适配或自监督后可改善。单纯增加Transformer参数量对精算表格任务带来的收益有限。

Conclusion: 在精算表格任务上，有效缩放不仅取决于数据和参数量的增加，更取决于架构设计和损失函数目标设计。研究结果可为不同数据规模下的模型选择提供定量依据，并表明Transformer若不配合恰当的归纳偏置和适配策略，其规模扩展优势在该类任务中难以兑现。

Abstract: Scaling laws in modern deep learning describe how held-out loss improves as model capacity, training data, and compute increase, often following power-law trends. We investigate whether analogous scaling regularities arise in actuarial ratemaking, where data are tabular, heterogeneous, and noisy, and where classical models such as GLMs remain strong baselines. Using a real-world motor insurance portfolio, we train models from different families across increasing fractions of the training data and multiple random seeds, evaluating out-of-sample Poisson deviance, a likelihood-based loss for Poisson count predictions in which lower values indicate better held-out fit. We find that all model families improve with additional data, but scaling exponents differ substantially: TabM exhibits markedly stronger data scaling than purely supervised tabular Transformers and standard MLP baselines. Transformer variants show weak parameter scaling unless augmented with additional inductive biases (TabM-style adaptation or self-supervision). These results provide quantitative guidance on model selection by data regime and suggest that effective scaling on actuarial tabular tasks depends on architecture and loss function objective design, with simple increases in Transformer size providing limited gains.

</details>


### [115] [Kernel Reboot: Breaking the Boundaries of Neural Tangent Kernels for Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03117)
*Amir Mallak,Alaa Maalouf,Lior Wolf,Daniela Rus,Dan Rosenbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三种算法(NTK-KIP、MetaQuill、MetaQuill-KIP)，将神经正切核(NTK)回归与神经场(NF)结合，实现从稀疏观测中进行快速高质量的极少量样本重建与图像修复，其中MetaQuill-KIP通过KIP风格非线性热启动结合元学习初始化的小偏移微调，在极稀疏观测下取得高PSNR和语义合理的修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经场能从连续坐标映射到颜色或密度等信号，但从稀疏观测中实现快速高质量重建仍然困难。经典NTK回归虽能给出闭式拟合解，但本质是线性的，无法积累可复用的任务先验，两者之间存在明显的能力缺口。

Method: 提出三个算法：(1) NTK-KIP学习蒸馏支持集坐标(及可选标签)，使有限NTK能从少量观测数据修补大块缺失区域，得到紧凑的非线性表示而非原始核求解；(2) MetaQuill通过元学习共享初始化，使新场景只需更新少量任务特定的权重偏移即可适配，实现真正的特征学习与可复用先验；(3) MetaQuill-KIP融合两者，先用KIP风格非线性热启动种子化任务，再围绕元学习初始化仅精修该小偏移。

Result: MetaQuill-KIP在极稀疏观测下实现了高PSNR重建与语义合理的图像修补，每实例仅需轻量适配。相比依赖大规模预训练生成先验且需昂贵逐图调参的扩散类基线，本方法无需此类重型依赖即可获得更优效果。

Conclusion: 该研究表明NTK驱动的神经场可以被同时用于非线性建模与元学习，弥合了解析核方法与实际少样本重建任务之间的差距，为极少量观测下神经场的高效重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Neural fields (NFs) map continuous coordinates to signals such as color or density, but fast high-quality reconstruction from sparse observations remains difficult. Classical Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) regression gives closed-form fits, yet it is fundamentally linear and cannot accumulate reusable task priors. We develop three algorithms that address these gaps. NTK-KIP learns a distilled support set of coordinates (and optional labels) so that a finite NTK can inpaint large missing regions from little observed data, yielding a compact non-linear representation instead of a raw kernel solve. MetaQuill meta-learns a shared initialization for an INR so that new scenes can be adapted by updating only a small task-specific weight offset, which provides true feature learning and a reusable prior. Finally, MetaQuill-KIP fuses both ideas: it seeds the task with a KIP-style non-linear warm start, then refines only that small offset around the meta-learned initialization. MetaQuill-KIP achieves high-PSNR reconstructions and semantically plausible inpainting under very sparse observations, while requiring only lightweight per-instance adaptation, whereas diffusion-style baselines typically depend on large pretrained generative priors and costly per-image tuning. This shows that NTK-driven neural fields can be made both non-linear and meta-learnable, narrowing the gap between analytic kernels and practical few-shot reconstruction.

</details>


### [116] [Routing Is Not Enough: Diagnosing Intra-Adapter Subspace Contention in MoE+LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03150)
*Mehreen Hossain Chowdhury,Nowshin Mahjabin,Ahmed Shafin Ruhan,Md Azam Hossain,Abu Raihan Mostofa Kamal,Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑多领域微调中MoE+LoRA假设（即token级路由可分离领域更新），通过代码+生物医学文本+数学推理实验发现路由分离無法防止负迁移。提出Jaccard路由重叠与adapter梯度余弦相似度两种诊断指标，定位干扰源于低秩adapter子空间内近正交梯度竞争。提出SpawnLoRA动态在MoE专家内部添加门控子adapter，有效减少负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多领域微调常将MoE路由与LoRA结合，默认假设token级路由可以分离领域特定的更新。作者希望检验这一假设是否成立，并探究路由分离是否足以防止不同领域间的负迁移。

Method: 1) 在Python代码+生物医学文本+数学推理三个领域上进行MoE+LoRA实验；2) 引入两种诊断指标：Jaccard路由重叠（衡量专家共享）和adapter梯度余弦相似度（衡量更新兼容性）；3) 提出SpawnLoRA方法，在检测到adapter级别竞争时于MoE专家内部动态添加带门控的子adapter，保持路由器固定不变。

Result: 尽管领域间专家路由几乎不相交（near-disjoint），添加生物医学数据仍显著增加代码困惑度。诊断表明干扰主要来自近正交的领域梯度在相同低秩adapter子空间内竞争。SpawnLoRA在Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct和OLMoE-1B-7B多个混合设置下有效减少负迁移，优于标准LoRA与rank-adaptive LoRA。

Conclusion: 专家内部的（spatial）结构分离带来的收益超越了单纯路由分离或秩扩展，说明多领域微调中需要在adapter子空间层面主动隔离竞争梯度，而仅依赖路由或加大秩不足以解决负迁移问题。

Abstract: Multi-domain fine-tuning often combines MoE routing with LoRA, assuming that token-level routing separates domain-specific updates. We test this assumption in MoE+LoRA using Python code paired with biomedical text and mathematical reasoning. Although these domains show near-disjoint expert routing, adding biomedical data substantially increases code perplexity, indicating that routing separation alone may not prevent negative transfer. To localize the failure, we introduce Jaccard routing overlap and adapter-gradient cosine similarity, which measure expert sharing and update compatibility, respectively. These diagnostics indicate that interference arises mostly from nearly orthogonal domain gradients competing within the same low-rank adapter subspace. We address this issue with SpawnLoRA, which dynamically adds gated sub-adapters inside MoE experts when adapter-level contention is detected, while keeping the router fixed. We evaluate SpawnLoRA on Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct and OLMoE-1B-7B across multiple mixture settings and find that it effectively reduces negative transfer compared with standard and rank-adaptive LoRA. These results demonstrate that structural separation inside experts provides benefits beyond routing or rank expansion alone.

</details>


### [117] [Frontier LLMs are effective batch optimizers: Assessing reasoning models in continuous and discrete settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03177)
*Frank Hu,Shriram Chennakesavalu,David Graff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了前沿大语言模型（LLMs）作为批量优化器在连续和离散优化设置中的表现。研究发现，LLMs在数值测试函数的零样本批量优化中与经典方法相当，但性能较为脆弱；然而在语义丰富、结构与预训练数据相似的离散空间中，LLM的批量优化行为非常高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM凭借大规模预训练已被视为有吸引力的优化先验，但现代推理型LLM在批量优化场景中的有效性尚未得到充分探索，因此需要系统评估其在此类设置中的性能。

Method: 作者将当前一代前沿LLM作为批量优化器，在连续与离散两种优化设置中系统测试其性能，并与经典非LLM优化方法进行对比。

Result: LLM在数值测试函数的零样本批量优化中具有竞争力但相比经典优化方法性能脆弱；在语义丰富的离散空间中，LLM批量优化表现显著优于其他方法。

Conclusion: LLM作为批量优化器在其预训练数据结构相似的语义丰富离散空间中最有效，而在纯数值优化场景中不如经典方法稳健。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) have become attractive priors for optimization due to their large-scale pretraining that enables them to navigate a variety of optimization settings. However, the effectiveness of modern reasoning LLMs in batch optimization settings remains underexplored. Here we investigate the performance of the current generation of frontier LLMs as batch optimizers in both continuous and discrete settings. We find that while LLMs are competitive zero-shot batch optimizers for numerical test functions, their performance is brittle compared to classical non-LLM optimization approaches. However, LLM priors are significantly better in semantically rich settings, indicating that their batch optimization behavior is highly effective when navigating and reasoning over the discrete spaces most similar in structure to their pretraining data.

</details>


### [118] [Portable Causal Fairness Across Synthetic Data Generator Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03180)
*Steven Golob,Sikha Pentyala,Martine De Cock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统验证了DECAF公平性机制是否可跨生成器族迁移。作者将三种公平性定义移植到九个来自三个不相关家族（边际基、GAN、扩散模型，均含差分隐私变体）的生成器上，在不同形式隐私保证级别下完成了2,520次匹配对实验（Adult和COMPAS数据集）。结果表明该机制可普遍迁移，且新提出的因果扩散骨干在公平性和保真度上均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DECAF此前在单个非隐私GAN上证明了三种公平性定义可转化为生成器因果图上的三类边割问题，但该机制的普适性尚未验证——它是否仅适用于DECAF本身，还是源于因果分解这一更基本原理，是未被检验的问题。作者希望确认该公平性机制能否跨越多样化的生成器架构和隐私框架而成立。

Method: 作者将三种公平性定义（经因果边割形式化）从DECAF迁移至九个生成器，覆盖三个不相关家族：基于边际的模型、GAN和扩散模型，每个家族均包含带差分隐私的变体。同时引入一个新的因果扩散骨干架构。实验设置了三个正式隐私保证级别，在Adult和COMPAS数据集上进行了2,520次匹配对运行的系统比较，同时评估公平性、保真度以及裁剪操作对下游分类器的影响。

Result: 因果边割机制在所有生成器族中均可迁移；新提出的因果扩散骨干在所有测试家族中实现了最公平的发布结果，其保真度接近边际模型的水平。应用公平裁剪对保真度影响极小，仅使下游分类器AUC平均下降约0.07至0.15。此外，增加隐私保证不会导致数据变得更不公平。

Conclusion: DECAF的公平性机制并非该特定GAN框架的特有产物，而是源于因果因子化本身的普适原理，可跨生成器架构和隐私框架迁移。因果扩散模型作为公平合成数据生成新的强基线，能够在保持高保真度的同时提供最优公平性，且公平性与差分隐私保障可以兼得。

Abstract: When a statistical agency or regulator releases synthetic data in place of sensitive records, it chooses the generator that produces the table, and can shape that generator so unfair pathways are absent. DECAF made this concrete on one non-private GAN: three fairness definitions become three sets of edge cuts on the generator's causal graph. Whether the mechanism belongs to DECAF, or to causal factorisation itself, was untested. We port all three definitions to nine generators from three unrelated families (marginals-based, GAN, and diffusion, each with differentially private variants), across three levels of formal privacy guarantee, over 2,520 matched-pair runs on Adult and COMPAS datasets. The mechanism transfers everywhere, and our new causal diffusion backbone yields the fairest release of any family we tested, at fidelity close to the marginals tier. Applying the cut barely moves fidelity, only costs a downstream classifier about $0.07$ to $0.15$ AUC on average, and adding privacy guarantees don't make the data less fair.

</details>


### [119] [Language-encoded network topology enables large language models to reason about complex networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03229)
*Ucchwas Talukder Utsha,Sakib Mostafa,James Zou,Md Tauhidul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BioGlyph，一种将网络拓扑结构编译成语义可解释的角色语言（如枢纽、社区核心、跨社区连接器）的表示方法，使大型语言模型在不改变网络或模型的情况下完成结构推理任务，在20个网络基准上相比边列表、数值和习得表示最高提升26个百分点的系统准确率，并在酵母蛋白互作网络中揭示了生物学组织规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽在自然语言任务上表现出色，但当网络以边列表、句子或测量表形式呈现时，因需要从底层数据中推断结构语义，模型在回答网络结构推理问题（如中心性、桥梁连接、删除节点后结构变化）上表现不佳。现有方法缺乏一种既能保留网络拓扑信息又具备语义可解释性的网络表示。

Method: BioGlyph由两步组成：首先通过图划分与结构测量识别每个元素的结构角色（如枢纽、社区核心、跨社区连接器等）；然后利用固定规则将这些角色翻译为通用词汇表，生成包含结构角色、支持性证据和语义后果的可解释描述。该表示仅改变网络输入的表达形式，保持网络本身和LLM不变。

Result: 在跨越五个领域的二十个网络上，BioGlyph显著提升了开源LLM的结构推理准确率，相比基于边、数值和学习得到的表示，系统准确率最高提升26个百分点。消融实验表明增益来自在语义可解释的层面显式编码结构角色。该增益在密集、社区结构明显的网络中更为显著，在稀疏网络中收益较小。在芽殖酵母蛋白互作网络中，跨社区连接器基因富集必需基因，而外围蛋白则出现必需基因的耗竭。

Conclusion: BioGlyph提供了一种既可供语言模型也可供科学家使用的可解释的网络结构表示，将图结构与自然语言语义桥接，实现了更优的结构推理能力，并能帮助揭示潜在的系统组织规律（如生物网络的基因重要性分布）。

Abstract: Networks describe systems in biology and beyond, from protein interactions and social relationships to power grids and citation records. Reasoning about such systems requires understanding their structure: which elements are central, which connections bridge separate communities, and how it changes when elements are removed. Although large language models (LLMs) excel at natural language, they struggle with such questions when networks are given as edge lists, sentences or measurement tables, because their structural meaning must be inferred. Here we introduce BioGlyph, which compiles network topology into an interpretable and transferable language of structural roles. BioGlyph combines graph partitioning and structural measurements to identify roles such as hubs, community cores and cross-community connectors, and fixed rules to translate them into a universal vocabulary. The representation describes each element through its structural role, supporting evidence and semantic consequences, leaving both the network and the LLM unchanged. Across twenty networks spanning five domains, BioGlyph substantially improves open LLMs' ability to answer structural reasoning questions, outperforming edge-based, numerical and learned representations by up to 26 percentage points in system accuracy. Ablations show that the gain comes from explicitly encoding structural roles in semantically interpretable terms. The gain is more prominent in dense, community-structured networks and diminishes in sparse networks whose topology is more readily inferred from text. In a budding-yeast protein-interaction network, BioGlyph exposes biological organization: cross-community connectors are enriched for essential genes, whereas peripheral proteins are depleted. BioGlyph thus provides an interpretable representation for both language models and scientists to reason about network structure.

</details>


### [120] [The 2026 PNPL Competition: Word Classification and Efficient Cross-Subject Generalisation in LibriBrain100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03231)
*Francesco Mantegna,Gereon Elvers,Dulhan Jayalath,Gilad Landau,Tasha Kim,Miran Özdogan,Luisa Kurth,Teyun Kwon,SungJun Cho,Benjamin Ballyk,Alex Fung,Anna Greer,Pratik Somaiya,Christian Herff,Yorguin Mantilla Ramos,Hamza Abdelhedi,Karim Jerbi,Greg Farquhar,Brendan Shillingford,Mark Woolrich,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 2026 PNPL竞赛基于扩展的LibriBrain100数据集（新增32名受试者、约80小时个体内数据），设立两个互补的词分类任务赛道：Deep赛道追求个体内词分类最佳性能，Broad赛道聚焦跨受试者泛化，逐步减少微调数据至约10分钟，推动非侵入式脑机接口向临床应用迈进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2025年PNPL竞赛虽在语音检测和音素分类任务上取得显著成绩（F1-macro分别为95.6%和73.6%），但依赖的是单受试者约50小时的MEG数据，而实用型脑机接口必须能从几分钟的数据中泛化到新用户，而非依赖数小时的个体内数据。

Method: 发布扩展数据集LibriBrain100，包含32名新增受试者（每人约40分钟）及约80小时的额外个体内数据；将任务课程推进到词分类阶段，设计两个互补赛道：Deep赛道在规模化的个体内数据上追求词分类最佳性能，Broad赛道逐步减少受试者特定微调数据量（约40分钟→20分钟→10分钟）以评估跨受试者泛化能力。

Result: Broad赛道在约10分钟的微调数据条件下进行词分类评估，该时长处于临床可行范围内，是逐步接近可在严重瘫痪患者中恢复沟通能力的非侵入式BCI的重要里程碑。

Conclusion: 2026年PNPL竞赛通过多受试者数据集和双赛道设计，弥补了先前研究仅关注个体内规模的局限，将非侵入式语音解码从实验室级性能推向面向新用户的跨受试者泛化，为临床应用的BCI铺平道路。

Abstract: The ambition of the 2025 PNPL competition (Landau et al., 2025) was to launch a multi-year curriculum for non-invasive speech decoding. Designed to progress from foundational tasks toward the linguistic complexity required for a practical brain-computer interface (BCI), it set the stage with speech detection and phoneme classification tasks. Winning submissions reached F1-macro scores of 95.6% and 73.6% on the respective tasks (Elvers et al., 2026), highly significant advances. This success was built on the LibriBrain dataset (Özdogan et al., 2025), the largest within-subject MEG dataset recorded at the time with ${\sim}50$ hours of data for one subject. However, while within-subject scale drives strong decoding performance, a practical BCI must generalise to new users from minutes of data, not hours.
  The 2026 PNPL competition responds to this challenge with LibriBrain100 (Mantegna et al., 2026), an extended LibriBrain dataset with 32 additional subjects (${\sim}40$ minutes each) plus even more within-subject data (${\sim}80$ hours). Advancing the curriculum of tasks to focus on word classification, two complementary tracks are presented in this competition: the Deep track targets within-subject word classification at scale, aiming at the best possible performance; the Broad track targets cross-subject generalisation, progressively reducing the amount of subject-specific fine-tuning data from ${\sim}40$ to ${\sim}20$ to ${\sim}10$ minutes, a duration that falls within a clinically feasible range and brings us a step closer to a non-invasive BCI capable of restoring communication to people living with profound paralysis.

</details>


### [121] [B2B Customer Conversion Prediction: A Document Representation, Graph Theory, and CatBoost Driven Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03239)
*Tianqi Wang,Sheikh Shams Azam,Wan Eih Huang,Anton Wiranata,Christopher G. Brinton,Jan P. Allebach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对B2B一次性销售场景中购买周期长（数月甚至数年）的挑战，本文提出了一套从客户数据聚合、特征生成到转化预测的完整框架。采用多键聚合算法将个人联系人归并到B2B客户层级，并针对公司名称等非标准化键提出新型聚类架构以处理拼写错误和变体等不规则问题；随后定义并生成特征集，应用CatBoost模型进行客户转化预测，最终达到91%的预测准确率，并基于预测结果讨论个性化营销活动的推荐策略以促进转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在B2B一次性销售情境下，购买周期可能长达数月甚至数年，长期过程中精准识别高潜力客户并推荐个性化营销活动对有效营销至关重要。现有方法缺乏针对B2B客户层级的数据聚合方案，尤其是面对公司名称等非标准化字段中存在的拼写错误与拼写变体问题，难以实现准确的客户级预测。

Method: 首先提出基于多个键将个人联系人聚合到B2B客户层级的算法；针对公司名称等非标准化键，设计新型架构进行聚类以处理拼写错误和拼写变体等不规则情况；随后定义并生成一组客户特征，应用CatBoost梯度提升模型进行客户转化预测（即是否进入销售漏斗）；最后基于预测结果与模型分析讨论个性化营销活动推荐策略。

Result: 该框架在B2B客户转化预测上达到91%的预测准确率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的端到端框架有效解决了B2B客户聚合、特征生成与转化预测问题，在取得高准确率的基础上，可进一步依据预测结果和模型分析来推荐个性化营销活动以促进客户向销售漏斗的转化。

Abstract: In the one-time selling B2B context, the buying cycle may last months or even years. During the long process, targeting customers that have a high potential to make purchases and recommending personalized campaigns accordingly are important for effective marketing. For this goal, we study the following problems, B2B customer data aggregation, customer feature generation, and prediction of whether a B2B customer would show interest in making a purchase (i.e., prediction of conversion into sales funnel). We propose an algorithm to aggregate individual contacts to the B2B customer level based on multiple keys. For non-standardized keys such as company names, we propose a novel architecture to cluster them in a domain encompassing irregularities such as spelling mistakes and spelling variants. We then define and generate a set of features and apply the CatBoost model for customer conversion prediction. Our framework achieves 91\% prediction accuracy. Based on the prediction results and analysis of the model, we then discuss personalized campaign recommendations to foster conversion.

</details>


### [122] [FlowBalance: Verifier-Grounded Self-Improvement from On-Policy Reasoning Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03241)
*Zixun Huang,Kishan Panaganti,Haitao Mi,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowBalance是一种基于验证器（verifier）的自我改进方法，通过将同策略轨迹的自我引导分数与验证器派生的组优势进行校准，实现对完整响应的归一化分布学习，在数学推理任务上优于FlowRL且训练更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型可以通过自身在线经验（on-policy experience）进行自我改进，但这一内循环是脆弱的：终端验证器提供的监督可靠但稀疏，而密集的同模型引导（same-model guidance）可能强化错误信心或将学习过度集中于狭窄的解决方案模式。因此需要一种既保留验证器可靠性、又能提供密集引导的方法。

Method: FlowBalance通过以下方式实现：1）对每个同策略轨迹，使用冻结的训练时策略视图（利用特权上下文）产生token级对数概率增益，聚合成轨迹级自我引导分数；2）用验证器派生的组优势校准该分数——正优势轨迹保留引导、负优势轨迹反转引导、无结果偏好时禁用引导；3）能量函数对参考策略进行指数重加权，通过轮廓轨迹平衡（profiled trajectory balance）使用每组rollout一个log-partition估计拟合归一化目标。

Result: 在数学推理任务上，FlowBalance在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上均优于FlowRL的平均性能，同时提升了训练速度和稳定性；避免了直接OPSD的响应长度坍缩问题；在AIME24受控诊断中表现出更高的正确策略多样性。

Conclusion: FlowBalance通过轨迹平衡实现了结果校准的自我引导（outcome-calibrated self-guidance），无需单独的token级模仿损失。理论分析建立了组内对比保持、最小变化反向KL表征、目标奖励的单调验证器控制，以及对被拒绝响应上的假阳性自我引导的精确修正。这为推理模型自我改进提供了一种既密集又可靠的训练范式。

Abstract: A reasoning model can improve from its own on-policy experience, but this inner loop is fragile: terminal verifiers provide reliable yet sparse supervision, while dense same-model guidance can reinforce false confidence or overconcentrate learning on a narrow solution mode. We introduce FlowBalance, a verifier-grounded self-improvement method that learns a normalized distribution over complete responses. For each on-policy trajectory, a frozen training-time view of the same policy uses privileged context to produce token-level log-probability gains, which are aggregated into a trajectory-level self-guidance score. FlowBalance calibrates this score with the verifier-derived group advantage: guidance is retained on positive-advantage trajectories, reversed on negative-advantage trajectories, and disabled when the rollout group provides no outcome preference. The resulting energy exponentially reweights a reference policy, and profiled trajectory balance fits the normalized target with one log-partition estimate per rollout group. This realizes outcome-calibrated self-guidance via trajectory balance, without a separate token-level imitation loss. Our analysis establishes within-group contrast preservation, a minimum-change reverse-KL characterization, monotonic verifier control of target reward, and an exact correction against false-positive self-guidance on rejected responses. On mathematical reasoning, FlowBalance improves average performance over FlowRL on both Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, while also improving training speed and stability, avoiding direct OPSD's response-length collapse, and exhibiting higher correct-strategy diversity in a controlled AIME24 diagnostic.

</details>


### [123] [Selective Hypergraph Refinement for Frozen Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03265)
*Zimo Si*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究冻结图聚类的后处理问题，提出选择性超图精炼（Selective Hypergraph Refinement, SHR）方法。该方法在模型、参数、节点表示和原始图结构均固定不变的前提下，利用属性超图补充高阶关系，生成候选残差方向并评估其可靠性，仅对具有充分支持的节点进行簇分配更新。实验表明在15个骨干-数据集组合中有13个取得正平均宏增益（宏增益0.066~0.137 pp），且平均仅改变约0.2%~0.4%的硬分配，表明冻结聚类输出仍存在有限但可测的改善空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图聚类方法通常通过优化模型参数和节点表示来提升性能，但对于已训练并冻结的模型，缺乏有效手段在聚类结果层面进一步改善。作者希望探索一种在冻结条件下，不更新模型参数、节点表示和图结构，仅通过后处理精化已有聚类分配的方法。

Method: SHR方法：(1) 利用属性超图补充普通图难以表达的高阶关系；(2) 从超图中生成候选残差方向；(3) 使用图结构、节点属性和匹配零模型证据评估候选方向的可靠性；(4) 仅对具有充分支持的节点更新簇分配，其余节点保留原分配。理论分析表明节点是否改变簇由其原生分配间隔和精化方向强度共同决定。

Result: 受控通用套件评估中，15个骨干-数据集组合里有13个平均宏增益为正，1个无效果，1个为负。单元平均宏增益为0.066个百分点（95% bootstrap CI [0.030, 0.107]），平均仅0.209%的硬分配发生改变。更广泛的15组合原生接口评估获得0.137个百分点的宏增益，平均改变率为0.375%。

Conclusion: 冻结聚类输出在训练后仍保留有限但可测量的精化空间；SHR通过选择性地应用超图精炼可取得正增益，但效果在不同骨干-数据集组合间呈异质性，且更广泛的覆盖会增加负迁移暴露的风险。

Abstract: Existing graph-clustering methods typically improve clustering performance by optimizing model parameters and node representations. Effective means of further improving the clustering results of an already trained and frozen model, however, remain limited. We study post-processing for frozen graph clustering. After checkpoint fixation, the procedure uses no labels and updates neither model parameters, node representations, nor the original graph structure.
