The federated environment offers an attractive option to balance privacy and causal discovery accuracy. Unfortunately, the standard centralised estimator in the LiNGAM setting, i.e., DirectLiNGAM, cannot be straightforwardly federated. Higher-order cumulant tensors offer a way around this obstacle: they depend only on the joint distribution of the variables involved and add exactly across independent sample groups, so a single communication round suffices in horizontal, vertical, and hybrid partitions.
  However, FedISHC, i.e., the current federated method along these lines, breaks down under near-symmetric noise. To overcome the above limitation, we introduce the FedRCD family of causal discovery algorithms, and investigate three variants that trade off communication rounds against algebraic noise; two of them are exact federated counterparts of the centralised high-order cumulant (HC) and HC-LiNGAM algorithms, and the single-round variants further effectively support exact unlearning at any granularity, from a single observation to a whole client. Numerical experiments show that at sample sizes typical of real deployments, the entire cumulant-based federated family does not actually rank variables by the population asymmetry that the scores encode at zero. It ranks them by a variance ladder induced by the DAG along its directed paths, the cumulant counterpart of varsortability. Marginal standardisation collapses every cumulant method to near-random ordering, while scale-invariant DirectLiNGAM, not federable under this protocol, is unaffected.

</details>


### [156] [From Nowcasting to Forecasting: Adapting a Reanalysis-Trained](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03763)
*Mikko Partio,Leila Hieta,Ossi Laine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CloudCast v2是一种用于12小时云量预报的机器学习模型，采用观测（卫星）初始条件，基于Copernicus欧洲区域再分析数据训练学习云演变动力学，并通过条件流匹配生成方法适配到卫星云场。相比前代CloudCast v1，MAE降低10%，在3-6小时后FSS评分超越v1，表明观测初始化的ML预报可扩展到传统临近期预报（1-3小时）之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期云量预报在初始几小时内能保留观测到的云分布，但当云场经历生成、消散和形变时技巧下降；更长的预报时效需要考虑大气演变，但业务NWP预报在初始化时无法准确再现卫星观测到的云状态。因此需要在观测初始条件下实现更长时效（12小时）的云量预报。

Method: CloudCast v2采用两阶段训练策略：首先在Copernicus欧洲区域再分析数据上训练以学习云演变动力学，然后利用条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）这一生成式方法，将噪声变换为云量预报，并以观测到的初始云场和NWP输入为条件进行适配。该方法结合了再分析数据的物理动力学与卫星云场的空间细节。

Result: 在1-12小时预报范围内，CloudCast v2相比前代CloudCast v1将平均绝对误差（MAE）降低了10%；在分数技巧评分（FSS，一种基于邻域的空间一致性度量）方面，根据云量类别不同，在约3-6小时后超越CloudCast v1。

Conclusion: 观测初始化的机器学习云量预报能够超越通常1-3小时的临近期预报范围，同时保留来自卫星云场的空间细节，验证了生成式条件流匹配方法在云演变预报中的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate cloud-cover forecasts are important for temperature prediction, radiation forecasting, and solar-power operations. Short-range forecasting methods can preserve observed cloud placement during the first forecast hours, but their skill decreases when cloud fields evolve through formation, dissipation and deformation. Longer lead times require accounting for atmospheric evolution, but operational numerical weather prediction (NWP) forecasts may not accurately represent the satellite-observed cloud state at initialization. We develop CloudCast v2, a machine-learning model for 12-hour cloud-cover forecasting from observation-based initial conditions. The model is first trained on the Copernicus European Regional Reanalysis (Ridal2024) to learn cloud-evolution dynamics, and is then adapted to satellite-derived cloud fields using conditional flow matching (Lipman2023), a generative method that transforms noise into cloud-cover forecasts conditioned on the observed initial cloud fields and NWP inputs. CloudCast v2 reduces mean absolute error by 10% relative to its predecessor, CloudCast v1 (Partio2025), over the 1-12 h range. It also overtakes CloudCast v1 in fractions skill score, a neighborhood-based measure of spatial agreement, after approximately 3-6 h, depending on the cloudiness category. These results show that observation-initialized machine-learning forecasts can extend beyond the usual 1-3-hour nowcasting range while retaining spatial detail from satellite cloud fields.

</details>


### [157] [OBER+: Continuity-Aware Reporting and Traceable Continuous Improvement in Outcome-Based Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03770)
*Elakkiya Rajasekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OBER+是一个对已部署的院校学习成果达成度平台（OBER）的扩展系统，它通过五个相连的阶段将"测量到的差距"自动转化为"评估后的纠正行动"：跨授课累积达成度、信号识别差距及持续差距、按监管机构已有阈值定级、将决策记录到带证据标注的实践目录、记录变更并量化差距的后续变化。另外，OBER+还引入了一条规则用于比较学习成果的先后版本表述，避免在成果变更点之后仍按同一序列解读达成度。在两条真实课程记录上的应用产生了三项发现：核心课程所有成果在连续两次交付间均被实质改写，若按字面理解会造成25个百分点的虚假崩塌；六成平台数据与文档规则复核不一致，暴露出一个已上报的缺陷；15对成果表述中5对逐字符相同，其余10对中有6对最接近的相似成果指向了不同编号的早期版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践结果导向教育（OBE）的院校常规计算学习成果达成度，但课程分析综述表明缺乏证据说明这些计算结果如何真正指导决策——即从测量到的短降到评估后的纠正行动，这一步在现有系统中是缺失的。

Method: OBER+扩展了一个已部署的院校达成度平台，提出五个相连的计算阶段（累积达成度、短降与持续短降信号、按监管已有阈值定级、记录决策至证据目录、日志化变更并量化短降后续移动），并增加一条规则比较成果先后表述，使成果变更前后不被当作同一序列解读。所有逻辑均以任何达成度平台可实现的形式化规则给出。

Result: 对两门真实课程的实时记录应用规则得出三个结果：(1) 核心课程每项成果在连续交付间均被实质重定义，主题在成果编号间迁移，字面解读会误报25个百分点的虚假崩塌；(2) 按文档规则重算平台数据，60%（十分之六）与舍入误差所不能解释的偏差不符，模式指向并查出并上报了一个系统缺陷；(3) 15对成果表述中5对逐字符相同，其余10对中有6对的成果与不同编号的早期成果最相似——此结论仅依赖相似度排序，无需阈值或标注。

Conclusion: OBER+的贡献在于为基于成果的报告提供了一种可计算的设计——以任何达成度平台均可实现的规则呈现，并用真实院校记录中的证据展示了这些规则能揭示出用字面阅读或逐交付堆积数据无法察觉的问题（成果语义漂移、计算缺陷、成果再标识）。

Abstract: Institutions practising outcome-based education compute learning outcome attainment routinely, while reviews of curriculum analytics report an absence of evidence on how that computation informs decisions. This paper presents OBER+, an extension of a deployed institutional attainment platform that computes the step from a measured shortfall to an evaluated corrective action. Five connected stages accumulate attainment across deliveries of a course, signal a shortfall and a persistent shortfall, grade it on cutoffs the regulator already uses, record the decision against a catalogue of practices annotated with their evidence, log the change, and quantify the subsequent movement in the shortfall. A further rule compares successive statements of an outcome, so attainment is never read as a series across a point at which the outcome changed. Applying the rules to the live record of two real courses produced three results. Every outcome of a core course was substantively redefined between consecutive deliveries, with subject matter moving between outcome numbers, so a naive reading would have reported a twenty-five point collapse between quantities that do not refer to the same learning. Recomputing the platform's figures from its documented rule showed six of ten differing by more than rounding explains, in a pattern that identified a defect since reported to the institution. Across fifteen statement pairs from three transitions, five were identical character for character, and among the ten that were not, the outcome carrying a given number was nearest to a differently numbered earlier outcome in six, a result resting on an ordering of similarities and requiring no threshold and no labelling. The contribution is a computational design for outcome-based reporting, stated as rules any attainment platform can implement, with evidence of what they make visible in a live institutional record.

</details>


### [158] [Landmark-Based Discrimination of Injury-Associated Athlete-Sessions from Minute-Resolution Multimodal Football Monitoring Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03790)
*Evangelos Chatzidimitriou,Konstantinos Tserpes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种固定地标、每运动员-会话一个表征的方法，解决运动员监测数据中分钟级连续记录与会话级二元标签之间的粒度不匹配问题。在2020 SoccerMon数据集（48名精英女子足球运动员、3,743个运动员-会话、22个损伤相关会话）上评估多种表征与机器学习基准，结果ROC-AUC 0.367-0.607，不确定性大，总体判别能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动员监测数据按分钟连续采集，而损伤信息仅以整个训练/比赛会话为单位标注，这导致标签与特征在时间粒度上的不一致。若将会话级标签直接赋给每一分钟，等于假设损伤发生的确切时刻是已知的，然而实际在会话内部并不能确定损伤何时发生，从而造成建模问题。

Method: 提出固定地标（如10、20、30分钟）的建模框架，在每个地标处为每个运动员-会话构建一个表征，仅使用截至该地标的信息，并将会话级损伤标签作为目标。比较赛前、累积、动态及组合表征，采用运动员分离验证、运动员聚类bootstrap不确定性、共同队列敏感性分析、备选阴性运动员划分、等权重运动员加权，并使用逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost作为基准。

Result: 主要结果来自累积+动态逻辑回归，ROC-AUC范围0.367-0.607，PR-AUC范围0.0080-0.0150，跨地标表现波动大且不确定性宽；含赛前信息的表征在多个地标上点估计较高，但统计上仍不确定。数据集仅22个损伤相关会话来自5名运动员，样本高度不平衡且有限。

Conclusion: 该固定地标建模框架在概念上解决了会话级标签与分钟级数据的粒度不匹配问题，但实验结果表明模型判别能力有限且不确定性大，反映了精英女子足球中损伤预测的内在困难以及损伤样本稀缺的限制。框架本身为后续研究提供了更严谨的评估范式。

Abstract: Athlete monitoring data may be recorded minute by minute throughout a match or training session, while injury information may only indicate whether the entire session was injury-associated.
  This creates a modelling problem: assigning the same session-level label to every minute would imply that injury status is known at each exact time, even though within-session injury onset is unknown.
  Our novelty is a fixed-landmark, one-representation-per-athlete-session formulation that directly addresses this mismatch. Instead of labelling every minute, we construct one representation per athlete-session at each landmark using information observed up to that point. This keeps the target at the session level and avoids unsupported minute-level injury supervision.
  A landmark is a fixed time point within the same session, such as 10, 20, or 30 minutes. At each landmark, we assess whether the whole session is injury-associated or non-injury-associated and examine how discrimination changes as more within-session information becomes available.
  Using 2020 SoccerMon data, we analyse 3,743 athlete-sessions from 48 elite women's football athletes, including 22 injury-associated sessions from five athletes. We evaluate pre-session, cumulative, dynamic, and combined representations with athlete-disjoint validation, athlete-cluster bootstrap uncertainty, common-cohort sensitivity analysis, alternative negative-athlete fold allocations, equal-athlete weighting, and Logistic Regression, Random Forest, and XGBoost benchmarks.
  Primary CUM+DYN Logistic Regression yields ROC-AUC 0.367-0.607 and PR-AUC 0.0080-0.0150 across landmarks, with wide uncertainty. PRE-containing representations show higher point estimates at several landmarks but remain uncertain.

</details>


### [159] [From Ordered Bernoulli Levels to Critical-Line Geometry: Integer Quantization, Bernoulli Residual Phase, and Prime-Power Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03801)
*Y. Kenan Yılmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有序伯努利词核f(p,n,k)=p^k(1-p)^(n-k)及其逆整数水平集生成的几何结构。二元水平2^(-n)唯一选择p=1/2作为实数分裂无关锚点，其复延拓产生共轭对称的垂直几何。通过二次坐标Q(z)=z(1-z)=1/4+u^2将临界线零点纵坐标映射为整数水平分解L_k=N_k+delta_k，其中delta_k为周期性伯努利残差，进而圆化后以相位变量隔离gamma_k^2 mod 1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图从组合概率论的伯努利词核出发构建一种纯几何框架，在未引入任何zeta函数输入之前就先建立共轭对称的锚定结构。其深层动机是通过伯努利水平集与二次坐标的整数量子化，为临界线零点纵坐标提供一种精确的分解方案，将gamma_k^2分解为整数壳层与周期性残差，从而建立Dirichlet级数与Euler积组装之间的结构性联系。

Method: 论文方法包括：(1)利用伯努利词核水平集2^(-n)唯一锚定p=1/2；(2)通过补数保持的复延拓构造z=1/2±iu的共轭对称垂直几何；(3)引入二次坐标Q(z)=z(1-z)=1/4+u^2实现精确整数量子化；(4)对临界线零点纵坐标gamma_k诱导水平L_k进行最近整数分解L_k=N_k+delta_k；(5)经圆化Z_k=exp(2πi·delta_k)隔离gamma_k^2 mod 1的残差相位；(6)通过唯一因子分解将整数壳层解析为素生成器坐标；(7)用独立复指数s将构造提升至Dirichlet原子m^(-s)以连接级数与积两种组装。

Result: 论文得到以下结果：构造了独立于zeta函数输入的伯努利诱导共轭对称几何；确立了Q(z)=1/4+u^2的精确整数量子化方案；建立了L_k=N_k+delta_k的精确分解，其中delta_k为周期第一伯努利残差；通过圆化映射实现了gamma_k^2 mod 1作为残差相位变量的隔离；利用唯一因子分解在整数壳层中建立了素生成器坐标系统；展示了该构造在Dirichlet原子层面的统一提升机制。

Conclusion: 作者明确将此工作界定为结构分析而非证明工作：精确恒等式、经典zeta联系、数值控制与开放的条件性Weyl检验被严格区分对待。论文明确声明不主张Riemann猜想的证明，定位为对伯努利词核几何与zeta零点结构之间关系的探索性刻画。

Abstract: We study the ordered Bernoulli-word kernel f(p,n,k)=p^k(1-p)^(n-k) and the geometry generated by its inverse-integer level sets. The binary level 2^(-n) selects p=1/2 as the unique real split-independent anchor. Under complement-preserving complex continuation, the pair becomes z=1/2+iu and 1-z=1/2-iu, producing a conjugation-symmetric vertical geometry before any zeta-function input is introduced. The quadratic coordinate Q(z)=z(1-z)=1/4+u^2 has a sharp minimum at the central point and admits an exact integer quantization. For critical-line zero ordinates gamma_k, the induced levels L_k=1/4+gamma_k^2 are decomposed exactly as L_k=N_k+delta_k, where N_k is the nearest integer and delta_k is a periodic first-Bernoulli residual. Circularization gives Z_k=exp(2 pi i delta_k), isolating gamma_k^2 mod 1 as the residual phase variable. Unique factorization resolves the integer shells into prime-generator coordinates, while a distinct complex exponent s lifts the same construction to the Dirichlet atoms m^(-s), linking the Dirichlet-series and Euler-product assemblies. Exact identities, classical zeta connections, numerical controls, and open conditional Weyl tests are kept explicitly separate. No proof of the Riemann Hypothesis is claimed.

</details>


### [160] [Free Pause Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03807)
*John Langford,Nathan Godey,Giovanni Monea,Yoav Artzi,Harry Dong,Ying Fan,Gustavo de Rosa,Zheng Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种"自由暂停token"机制，通过并行预测流在权重共享骨干上为语言模型的每一步预测提供额外算力，而非增加序列中的额外token。该方法将下一个token预测的困惑度提升2-3个百分点，推理时几乎不增加长度、KV缓存或延迟，主要代价仅体现在训练阶段（约1.14倍训练算力），实现与标准transformer在同等算力、参数和token量下的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思考token（pause token）虽然能为模型提供额外计算来改进每个下一token预测，但作为序列中的额外token会带来推理阶段上下文长度、KV缓存和延迟的增加。作者希望获得思考token的好处，同时避免这些推理开销，即在权重共享骨干上通过并行预测流携带额外计算，而非序列中的显式token。

Method: 在语言模型中引入自由的暂停token，该token不添加到序列中，而是在现有位置上通过并行预测流进行运算；与思考token共享权重骨干，但计算发生在并行流而非序列内，因此不增加序列长度、KV缓存和延迟。训练时该机制需要额外的训练计算（相比优化后的预训练流程约为1.14倍）。

Result: 在1B参数模型上，该方法将下一token预测性能提升2-3个centinats（困惑度指标）。推理时无额外上下文长度、KV缓存开销，几乎无延迟增加，因为推理FLOPs增长通常不是吞吐量的活跃瓶颈。训练计算开销最低降至1.14倍，同时保留大部分收益。

Conclusion: 该方法在等FLOPs、等参数量、等token数的条件下实现了对标准下一token训练transformer的一致改进，是一种isoflop、isoparameter、isotoken的系统性提升方案。

Abstract: A free pause token gives a language model extra compute to form each next-token prediction (as a pause, or thinking, token does) but carries that compute in a parallel prediction stream over a weight-shared backbone rather than as an extra token in the sequence. It improves next-token prediction by 2-3 centinats in practice on a 1B parameter model. Because the pause rides an existing position instead of adding one, it is free to use: at inference it adds no context length, no KV cache, and essentially no latency with the growth in inference flops typically irrelevant as it is not the active bottleneck on throughput. The only primary cost is in training, where additional training compute versus an optimized pretraining pipeline is reduced to as low as x1.14 while preserving most of the benefits. The result is an isoflop, isoparameter, and isotoken improvement over standard next token trained transformers.

</details>


### [161] [A Peer-Relative Representation Learning Framework for Energy Inefficiency Identification in Mobile Network Sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03809)
*Eliud Nyakweba Koto,Jaco du Toit,Adham Stoltz,Johan du Preez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于无监督同行相对比较的移动网络站点能源低效检测方法，通过改进最小失真嵌入（MDE）算法，引入基于能量的排斥机制，将有异常高能耗的站点从局部邻居中分离出来，从而识别潜在低效站点并支持大规模能耗优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源消耗是移动网络运营商最大运营支出之一，但站点级能源低效问题（如故障冷却控制器、闲置无线电设备、寄生辅助负载）因缺乏真实标签和测量数据中已嵌入低效而难以检测。

Method: 提出无监督同行相对比较方法，假设结构和运营特征相似的站点能耗应相近。引入能量感知的最小失真嵌入（MDE）公式，在标准MDE目标基础上增加基于能量的排斥机制，使相对同行异常高能耗的站点在嵌入空间中偏离局部邻域，同时保留结构相似性并编码能耗偏差。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出方法优于传统异常检测基线，能为大规模移动网络能源效率优化提供稳健基础。

Conclusion: 通过同行相对比较产生的异常分数为现场调查优先级提供实用机制，帮助移动网络运营商将工程资源集中在最可能实现节能的站点上。

Abstract: Energy consumption is one of the largest operational expenditure items for mobile network operators, yet site-level energy inefficiencies such as faulty cooling controllers, idle radio equipment, and parasitic auxiliary loads often remain undetected because no ground-truth inefficiency labels exist and historical measurements may already contain embedded inefficiencies. This study proposes an unsupervised peer-relative approach based on the premise that sites with similar structural and operational characteristics should exhibit comparable energy consumption. To capture these relationships, a novel energy-aware Minimum Distortion Embedding (MDE) formulation is introduced that extends the standard MDE objective with an energy-based repulsion mechanism. This encourages sites with anomalously high energy consumption relative to comparable peers to become displaced from their local neighbourhoods in the embedding space. The resulting low-dimensional representation simultaneously preserves structural similarity and encodes energy-related deviations, enabling the identification of potentially inefficient sites through peer-relative comparison. The derived anomaly scores provide a practical mechanism for prioritising field investigations, allowing mobile network operators to focus engineering resources on sites most likely to yield energy savings. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms conventional anomaly detection baselines and provides a robust foundation for large-scale energy-efficiency optimisation in mobile networks.

</details>


### [162] [Witnesses Explain Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03826)
*Lamine Diop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WAND是一种可解释的、无监督的表格数据异常检测器，其设计内在具备可解释性——用于标记异常点的"目击方向"（witness directions）天然构成该点的逐特征归因解释。WAND在47个ADBench数据集上达到了最佳平均Friedman排名，在ROC-AUC上与16个无监督基线持平，而原生解释在准确性和忠实度上超过SHAP/LIME及ECOD，且查询成本极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流无监督异常检测器只给出分数而不说明是哪些特征驱动了异常判定，解释通常通过SHAP或LIME事后强行附加，这种方式需要为每个点反复查询检测器数千次且只是近似。亟需一种"内在可解释"的检测器，能在打分的同时零成本得到逐特征归因，且不牺牲检测精度。

Method: WAND将计算组织为围绕单位球面上的方向进行。每个点按其投影偏离亚高斯极值基线的程度打分；关键的创新在于，标记某点的"目击方向"（特征空间中的向量）本身就是该点的解释，构成了逐特征归因，且由于分数可微，可进一步通过梯度恢复归因。打分的计算复杂度与样本量呈线性关系，并通过"探测效率界"（probe-efficiency bound）保证每个异常点都能获得目击方向从而获得解释。

Result: 在47个ADBench数据集上，WAND在ROC-AUC上与16个无监督基线持平的情况下取得了最佳平均Friedman排名；其原生解释比事后附加的SHAP/LIME以及ECOD更准确、更忠实，且查询成本仅为其极小一部分。

Conclusion: WAND表明在表格数据上可以实现"可解释性零精度损失"的目标：其在获得与最先进无监督检测器同等检测性能的同时，天然携带准确且忠实的逐特征解释，是一种实用、可解释的异常检测解决方案。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection scores each point of an unlabelled, contaminated sample in a single pass, and increasingly must also explain why a point is flagged. Yet the dominant detectors give a score with no account of which features drive it, and explanations are bolted on post-hoc with SHAP or LIME, which re-query the detector thousands of times per point and only approximate it. We introduce WAND, an unsupervised tabular anomaly detector that is explainable by design. WAND organises its computation around directions on the unit sphere, scoring each point by how far its projection escapes a sub-Gaussian extreme-value baseline. The originality of our approach is that the witness directions that flag a point, being vectors in feature space, are its explanation, a per-feature attribution obtained at no cost over scoring and, since the score is differentiable, recoverable by gradients. Scoring is linear in the sample size, and a probe-efficiency bound guarantees every anomaly a witness, hence an explanation. Across 47 ADBench datasets WAND attains the best mean Friedman rank at ROC-AUC parity with 16 unsupervised baselines, so the gain is interpretability at no accuracy cost; its native explanations are more accurate and faithful than post-hoc SHAP/LIME and ECOD at a fraction of the query cost. WAND is thus a practical, interpretable solution for explainable anomaly detection.

</details>


### [163] [Multi-step Proximal Policy Improvement in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03842)
*Soohyun Choi,Seonvin Cho,Songnam Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多步近端策略改进（MPI），通过几何流视角将离线RL中的演员更新统一理解为流形上的近端策略改进步骤，并在此基础上构建re-centered的顺序近端更新机制，使策略能在可控范围内超越数据集支持并提升离线RL性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习面临一个核心矛盾：策略更新需要保持在数据集支持的范围内以保证价值估计可靠，但真正的性能提升往往需要策略超越行为分布。缺乏一个统一的几何框架来理解和衔接这两个诉求。

Method: 将策略建模为带度量几何的概率流形，将一类广泛的离线actor目标解释为单一近端策略改进步骤（SPI），即由critic能量诱导的流形梯度流的隐式离散化。在此基础上提出MPI，通过组合顺序的re-centered近端步骤实现受控的多步策略改进，支持确定性策略和对角高斯策略等多种策略几何。

Result: 在D4RL基准上，少量的MPI细化步骤即可在多个任务上提升强离线baseline（TD3+BC、ReBRAC、IQL）的性能。进一步的诊断实验区分了re-centered细化与固定目标更新调度的差异，并刻画了critic误差下的局限性。

Conclusion: MPI为离线策略改进提供了一种即插即用的细化机制，在享受近端控制的保障下实现超越数据集支持的可控策略改进，并具有理论上的几何一致性与实践上的通用性。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) must reconcile two competing requirements: policy updates should stay near dataset-supported actions to keep value estimates reliable, yet meaningful gains often require moving beyond the behavior distribution. We develop a geometric view of offline actor updates by modeling policies as a probability manifold endowed with a chosen metric geometry. Under this lens, a broad class of offline actor objectives can be interpreted as a single proximal policy improvement step (SPI), i.e., an implicit discretization of a manifold gradient flow induced by a critic-defined energy. Building on this insight, we propose multi-step proximal policy improvement (MPI), a plug-in refinement mechanism that composes sequential re-centered proximal steps. MPI enables controlled policy improvement beyond dataset support while retaining proximal control at each refinement. The framework accommodates multiple policy geometries and admits practical instantiations for deterministic and diagonal-Gaussian policies. Experiments on D4RL benchmarks show that small numbers of MPI refinements improve strong offline baselines, including TD3+BC, ReBRAC, and IQL, on many tasks. Focused diagnostics further distinguish re-centered refinement from fixed-objective update scheduling and characterize limitations under critic error.

</details>


### [164] [Pushing the (Decision) Boundaries: Dynamically Calibrating Differentially Private Noise to Explainability in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03851)
*Michael Khavkin,Kichang Lee,Jaeho Jin,JeongGil Ko,Eran Toch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出XCal-FL，一种闭环、可解释性驱动的联邦学习本地训练算法，通过三种互补信号（预测logit变化、反事实边界、显著性集中度）动态校正差分隐私噪声，在保持形式化DP保证的同时提升模型准确率（>10%）和解释保真度（最高5倍），揭示解释保真度是隐私权衡中独立于效用之外的独特维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习结合差分隐私虽能保护数据机密性，但DP噪声会扭曲学习表征并降低解释保真度，这在需要可信解释的任务（如临床辅助诊断）中构成瓶颈。现有方法将DP噪声适应静态特征重要性信号，仅局限于事后分析，无法在训练过程中根据解释质量进行噪声校准。

Method: 提出XCal-FL闭环可解释性驱动的本地训练算法：从三个互补信号动态校准DP噪声——(1)预测logit变化，衡量对模型置信度的因果影响；(2)反事实边界，捕捉决策边界敏感性；(3)显著性集中度，量化模型注意力空间一致性；并通过自适应隐私核算保证形式化DP。

Result: 在三个医学影像数据集和多种FL配置下，XCal-FL相比静态噪声FL将预测性能提升超10%，解释保真度提升最高5倍；优于现有最先进自适应DP方法。隐私预算效率更高，每个累积隐私损失单位带来准确率和解释保真度的更大增益。分析还发现预测性能随隐私损失近似线性增长，而解释保真度呈非线性动态。

Conclusion: 解释保真度是隐私权衡中独立于效用的独特维度，无法仅从效用推断。这对决策关键应用中训练与隐私预算分配具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) with Differential Privacy (DP) is increasingly adopted to preserve data confidentiality in distributed machine learning. However, DP noise distorts learned representations and degrades explanation fidelity, limiting differentially private FL where trustworthy explanations are required, such as assistive clinical diagnosis. Prior work adapted DP noise with static feature-importance signals, restricting explainability to post hoc analysis and precluding noise calibration to explanation quality during training. We propose XCal-FL, a closed-loop, explainability-driven local training algorithm for image classification in cross-silo FL that dynamically calibrates DP noise from three complementary signals: (1) prediction logit variations, measuring causal influence on model confidence, (2) counterfactual margins, capturing decision-boundary sensitivity, and (3) saliency concentration, quantifying spatial coherence of model attention, while enforcing formal DP guarantees via adaptive privacy accounting. Experiments on three medical imaging datasets across varying FL configurations show that XCal-FL yields more accurate and interpretable global models, improving predictive performance by over 10\% and explanation fidelity by up to 5$\times$ over static-noise FL, and outperforming state-of-the-art adaptive DP methods in fidelity. XCal-FL also achieves higher privacy-budget efficiency, turning each unit of cumulative privacy loss into larger gains in both accuracy and explanation fidelity. Our analysis further reveals that, unlike predictive performance, which scales roughly linearly with privacy loss, explanation fidelity exhibits non-linear dynamics. These findings suggest explainability is a distinct dimension of the privacy trade-off that cannot be inferred from utility alone, with implications for training and privacy-budget allocation in decision-critical applications.

</details>


### [165] [Differentiable Interval Bottlenecks for Interpretable Anomaly Detection in Numerical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03878)
*Lamine Diop,Marc Plantevit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DIFFINT，一种具有可解释瓶颈结构的自编码器异常检测方法。其潜在瓶颈由一组端到端学习的软轴对齐区间隶属度构成，每个潜变量对应特征空间中的可读超矩形。在48个ADBench基准上对比22个基线，DIFFINT在两个指标上均获得最佳平均排名，且是统计学并列领先七强中唯一的可解释检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于重构的异常检测器（如深度自编码器）精度高但缺乏可解释性——它们能识别异常样本，却无法告知实践者哪些特征范围导致了异常。现有方法无法在保持深度表示学习能力的同时提供可检查的内部结构。

Method: DIFFINT将自编码器的潜在瓶颈构建为一组软性、轴对齐的区间隶属度，直接从原始数值数据端到端学习，无需离散化或二值化。每个潜变量单元对应一个可读的、特征空间中的超矩形；实例通过其在各区间内的隶属强度编码，重构误差作为异常分数。通过Lipschitz约束解码器实现认证的重构误差下界，并提供闭式、无标签的(单元,特征)对重要性排名以审计学习到的约束。

Result: 在48个ADBench基准上对比22个基线（统一的[-1,1]归一化协议），DIFFINT在ROC-AUC（4.10）和AUPR（4.16）上均取得最佳平均排名；在纯内点训练检测器中显著领先其所在类别，并与最强的含污染数据检测器竞争（见分层与完整案例分析）。它是统计学并列领先七方法集群中唯一的可解释检测器。

Conclusion: DIFFINT证明可解释性与异常检测性能可以兼得：其区间结构不仅提供了审计能力（闭式重要性排名、认证误差下界、分级抑制机制），还在广泛基准上达到最先进的检测精度，是唯一进入领先统计集群的可解释方法。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based anomaly detectors are accurate but opaque: a deep autoencoder flags a sample without telling a practitioner which feature ranges made it anomalous. We propose DIFFINT, an autoencoder whose latent bottleneck is structured as a set of soft, axis-aligned interval memberships learned end-to-end directly from raw numerical data, without any discretization or binarization. Each latent unit corresponds to a human-readable hyper-rectangle in feature space; an instance is encoded by how strongly it falls inside each interval relative to the other units, and its reconstruction error is the anomaly score. This keeps the power of differentiable representation learning while exposing an inspectable internal structure. We make the inductive bias precise: a certified reconstruction-error lower bound for points that fall outside every active coordinate of the learned support (with a Lipschitz-enforced decoder), and a graded, empirically verified suppression mechanism for the usual case in which only a few features are abnormal; and we provide a closed-form, label-free importance that ranks each (unit, feature) pair from quantities the model already maintains, turning trained intervals into auditable candidate constraints without ever seeing an anomaly label. On 48 ADBench benchmarks against 22 baselines under a common [-1, 1]-normalized protocol, DIFFINT attains the best mean rank overall on both metrics (4.10 on ROC-AUC, 4.16 on AUPR); among inlier-only detectors it leads its regime clearly, and it is competitive with the strongest contaminated-data detectors (see the stratified and complete-case analyses). It is the only interpretable detector in the statistically-tied leading cluster of seven methods.

</details>


### [166] [Beyond Endpoint Scores: Time- and Capacity-Conditioned Evaluation of Continual Knowledge Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03900)
*Heejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出持续知识更新方法的比较常局限于单一最终检查点和单一重放LoRA秩，这不足以识别更优工作点。通过在24个月Wikidata流上跨不同评估月份、重放LoRA秩和查询表述进行比较，发现排名会随评估区域翻转——周期性层级方法在rank-8时领先5.0分，在rank-72时反而落后11.6分。作者建议报告轨迹与容量扫描，仅当排序在评估区域内稳定时才声明稳健胜者，否则应报告胜者区域与保留-稳定性-成本边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续知识更新方法的比较方式过于简化——仅用单一最终检查点和单一常规adapter秩(trank)来判断优劣，这可能导致错误的结论。作者希望通过跨评估月份、重放LoRA秩和查询表述的系统性变化，揭示方法排序是否在这些维度上稳定。

Method: 在24个月的Wikidata数据流上，将固定周期性层级方法与累积重放方法进行比较。系统性地变化三个因素：评估月份（when）、重放LoRA秩（rank capacity）、查询表述。在Qwen2.5-1.5B、Llama-3.2-1B及抽取改写（held-out paraphrases）上进行验证。

Result: 方法排名在评估区域呈联合依赖：单点比较时层级方法显著领先，随重放秩提高排序反转。在Qwen2.5-1.5B上，对rank-8重放的5.0分优势变成对rank-72重放的11.6分劣势；在高秩时单一终点可能暗示平局，而时间平均重放领先9-13分。该秩条件反转在Llama-3.2-1B和改写集上复现。

Conclusion: 持续更新方法的比较应报告评估轨迹与容量扫描，而非单点结果。稳健胜者仅当排序在整个评估区域内稳定时才能声明；否则应报告胜者区域和保留-稳定性-成本边界。在此协议下，周期性层级被定位为低更新成本工作点，而非质量胜者。

Abstract: Continual knowledge-updating methods are often declared superior from one final checkpoint and one conventional adapter rank. We show that this can be insufficient to identify the better operating point. Holding a periodic hierarchy fixed, we compare it with cumulative replay over a 24-month Wikidata stream while varying evaluation month, replay LoRA rank, and query formulation. The apparent winner changes across this region: on Qwen2.5-1.5B, the hierarchy's 5.0-point advantage over rank-8 replay becomes an 11.6-point deficit against rank-72 replay, and at high ranks a consolidation-aligned endpoint can suggest a tie while time-averaged replay leads by 9-13 points. The same rank-conditioned reversal appears on Llama-3.2-1B and held-out paraphrases.
  These results show that method ranking in continual updating can depend jointly on when performance is measured and how much replay-side adaptation capacity the baseline receives. We therefore propose reporting trajectories and capacity sweeps, and declaring a robust winner only when the ordering is stable across the evaluation region; otherwise, comparisons should report winner regions and retention-stability-cost frontiers. Under this protocol, the periodic hierarchy is a lower-update-cost operating point, not a quality winner.

</details>


### [167] [RATL: Learning from Retrieved Residuals for Robust Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03937)
*Yuchen He,Yueyang Cang,Zhiyuan Ning,Ningyu Wang,Li Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RATL是一种面向多元时间序列预测的即插即用残差检索与反馈校正方法。它将基础预测器冻结以构建检索键，把历史预测残差转化为仅用于训练的模型专属记忆。推理时，RATL在因果可用性约束下检索与当前上下文相似的历史残差轨迹，并通过一个感知集合的路由器（覆盖预测块与变量）选择和组合这些轨迹，从而改进冻结基础预测器的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）已被证明能有效增强参数化模型，但同样的思路难以直接迁移到连续输出回归任务，因为直接复用检索到的目标值在样本输出水平、数值尺度或局部动态不同时往往不够鲁棒。此外，传统预测流程虽然用残差做模型优化和误差诊断，但并未将单个历史残差示例作为可在推理时访问的记忆保留下来。作者希望填补这两点空白。

Method: RATL首先冻结基础预测器以构造检索键，并将该基础模型的历史预测残差转化为仅用于训练的专属记忆库。推理阶段，在因果可用性约束下检索与当前上下文相似的历史残差轨迹，随后使用一个能感知集合的路由器，在预测块和变量维度上选择并组合检索到的残差轨迹，对基础预测器输出进行反馈校正。校正强度通过验证集进行选择，以防止残差过度注入。

Result: 实验表明，与当前上下文匹配的历史残差包含可复用的预测信息。在真实世界基准上以iTransformer作为主要冻结基础预测器，RATL在大多数实验设置中优于多个强预测基线，并验证了跨骨干架构的可迁移性。消融实验进一步证实：学习式的路由能增强原始残差反馈，而基于验证的校正强度选择可限制残差过度注入。

Conclusion: RATL将检索对象从历史目标值转变为基础模型专属的历史预测误差，为连续输出预测提供了一种即插即用、基于残差记忆的习得式反馈校正范式。该工作拓展了检索增强思想在回归任务中的应用边界。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) complements parametric models with retrieved external evidence. The same idea is attractive for continuous-output regression, but directly reusing retrieved target values is often not robust when samples differ in output level, numerical scale, or local dynamics. Moreover, conventional forecasting pipelines generally use residuals for model optimization and error diagnosis, but do not retain individual historical residual examples as memory that can be accessed at inference time.For multivariate time-series forecasting, we propose RATL, a plug-in residual-retrieval and feedback-correction method. RATL freezes a base forecaster to construct retrieval keys and turns its historical forecast residuals into a train-only memory specific to that base model. At inference time, RATL retrieves residual trajectories from similar historical contexts subject to causal availability constraints, then uses a set-aware router operating over forecast blocks and variables to select and combine these trajectories. Experiments show that historical residuals matched to the current context contain reusable forecasting information and that RATL improves frozen base forecasters in most experimental settings. Ablations further show that learned routing strengthens raw residual feedback, while validation-based correction-strength selection limits residual over-injection.On real-world benchmarks, we use iTransformer as the primary frozen base forecaster, compare against multiple strong forecasting baselines, and test transferability across backbones. The results show that RATL can further improve base-forecaster performance in most settings.Overall, RATL shifts the retrieved object from historical target values to base-model-specific historical forecast errors, providing a plug-in, residual-memory-based paradigm for learned feedback correction in continuous-output forecasting.

</details>


### [168] [Headroom-Drift Replay: A Primitive for Principled Replay Control in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03941)
*Hyun Bin Park,Du-Seong Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Headroom-Drift Replay，一种用于GRPO的组级replay控制原语，将轨迹复用分解为"排序"（按剩余学习价值排名）和"门控"（按与当前策略的兼容性筛选）两个决策，在不增加辅助生成或训练机制的情况下，仅通过原则化replay选择即可在数学推理、多模态推理和Agentic Search基准上超越朴素replay并匹敌更复杂的replay方法，同时显著降低Agentic Search中的端到端时间成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向推理模型的基于强化学习的后训练日益受限于频繁的全新rollout生成，尤其在智能体场景中环境交互主导了端到端时间成本。虽然replay可通过复用历史轨迹降低负担，但现有方法通常将其嵌入更大规模的训练管线中（涉及探索、经验重构或混合策略优化），导致replay自身贡献难以被单独评估。作者希望以聚焦的方式回答：仅靠原则化的replay选择本身能达到何种效果。

Method: 提出Headroom-Drift Replay，一种应用于GRPO的组级replay控制原语，将复用拆分为两个独立决策：(1) Headroom——依据存储组残余的学习价值进行排序；(2) Drift——依据与当前策略的兼容性对这些组进行门控筛选。新鲜的on-policy轨迹流保持不变，该方法不引入任何辅助生成或训练机制。

Result: 在数学推理、多模态推理和Agentic Search基准上，该单一干预在Avg Mean@32指标上优于朴素replay，并匹敌或超越更广泛的replay方法。在环境交互主导成本占比的Agentic Search场景中，它在保持相近质量的同时显著降低了端到端时间成本。

Conclusion: 仅靠原则化的replay选择（排序+门控）这一简单干预就足以取得有竞争力的性能提升，说明复杂的replay管线并非必要；Headroom-Drift Replay为需要控制计算成本的replay训练提供了一种轻量、可隔离分析的有效原语。

Abstract: RL-based post-training for reasoning models is increasingly bottlenecked by repeated fresh rollout generation, particularly in agentic settings where environment interaction dominates wall-clock cost. Replay can reduce this burden by reusing past trajectories, but existing methods typically embed it within larger training pipelines involving exploration, experience restructuring, or mixed-policy optimization. This makes replay's own contribution difficult to isolate. We ask a focused question: how far can principled replay selection alone go? We introduce Headroom-Drift Replay, a group-level replay control primitive for GRPO that separates reuse into two decisions. Headroom ranks stored groups by remaining learning value, while Drift gates them by compatibility with the current policy. The fresh on-policy stream remains unchanged, and the method adds no auxiliary generation or training machinery. Across mathematical reasoning, multimodal reasoning, and Agentic Search benchmarks, this single intervention outperforms naive replay and matches or exceeds broader replay methods on Avg Mean@32. In Agentic Search, where environment interaction dominates cost, it delivers comparable quality at materially lower wall-clock time.

</details>


### [169] [VestigeKV: The NoPE-MLA KV Cache Carries Its Own Eviction Signal in a Vestigial Branch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03949)
*WenJie Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VestigeKV，一种针对长寿命KV缓存压缩的方法。核心洞察是：在NoPE（无位置编码）训练的MLA模型中，模型将原本用于RoPE的64维解耦分支"重新利用"为显著性通道，从而提供与查询无关的逐token重要性信号。VestigeKV仅读取每行11%的维度即可对缓存分区：top-m行保留在注意层级，其余行精确移动（而非删除）到驻留GPU的归档中，可由认证触发条件按步检索。该方法无需训练、量化或修改权重/内核，在Kimi Linear上8x压缩时检索保持1.00，32x时0.92，零差距于全行选择；注意层级在32x时仅为每token缓存8.1KB中的0.25KB，归档保持逐位精确且GPU驻留。该机制对RoPE模型失效（相同操作退化到0.08），且证明了在RoPE下查询无关的精确合并不可能。所有阈值在数据前冻结，并附带20条已归档判决和8条封闭路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长寿命KV缓存必须在未来查询存在之前就被压缩，因此依赖观测注意力（如H2O、SnapKV）的逐token重要性选择方法在此场景失效——在NoPE MLA模型上针检索得分仅0.00-0.33，因为token的重要性尚未被观测到。需要一个与查询无关但能反映token固有重要性的驱逐信号。

Method: VestigeKV利用NoPE训练模型将64维解耦分支（RoPE的痕迹）重新用作显著性通道这一发现。仅读取每行11%的维度，将缓存分为两层级：top-m行进入注意层级，其余行精确移动到GPU驻留归档（而非删除），可通过认证触发条件逐步骤检索。支持两种变体：标准召回层级配置和以主机卸载回收VRAM的变体。所有阈值在测试数据之前冻结。

Result: 在Kimi Linear上：8x压缩下针检索保持1.00，32x下0.92（上下文8k至65k），与全行选择零差距。32x时注意层级仅占每token缓存8.1KB中的0.25KB；归档保持逐位精确且GPU驻留。标准配置（召回层级）在128x下仍达1.00。对RoPE MLA执行相同操作则崩溃至0.08（普通驱逐0.42）；证明RoPE下查询无关的精确合并不可能（top-1目标仅覆盖2.3-6.7%的token vs NoPE的10.2-46.8%）。

Conclusion: VestigeKV证明了在NoPE模型中可利用缓存自身携带的查询无关信号进行无损压缩，达成任何查询都读取全部token信息的存储效率。该方法对RoPE模型本质上不适用，因为旋转消灭了查询无关显著性的存在。作者报告称Kimi K3使用NoPE Gated-MLA变体，若缓存布局匹配则方法可能扩展，但作者不做超出所测模型的声称。

Abstract: The problem. A long-lived KV cache must be compressed before the queries that will read it exist; selection by observed attention (H2O, SnapKV) collapses there (0.00-0.33 needle retrieval on a NoPE MLA model), because a token's importance has not yet been observed. The method. On Kimi Linear, VestigeKV evicts by a query-independent signal the cache already carries: the 64-dimensional decoupled branch, a vestige of RoPE that NoPE training repurposes into a salience channel. Reading 11% of each row, it partitions the cache: the top-m rows stay in the attended tier; every other row moves -- exactly, never deleted -- to a GPU-resident archive reachable per step by a certified trigger. No training, no quantization, no weight or kernel change. Cost. Nothing measurable: retrieval holds at 1.00 under 8x and 0.92 under 32x from 8k to 65k context, zero gap to full-row selection. The attended tier is 0.25 KB of Kimi Linear's 8.1 KB per-token cache at 32x; the archive stays bit-exact and GPU-resident, with host offload as the VRAM-reclaiming variant. The recall tier -- the standard configuration -- holds 128x at 1.00. Kimi K3 is reported to use a NoPE Gated-MLA variant; if its cache layout matches, the method plausibly extends there -- we make no claim beyond the measured model. NoPE exclusivity. The identical operator on a RoPE MLA collapses to 0.08 (plain eviction: 0.42); query-independent salience itself exists only without rotation (top-1 targets span 2.3-6.7% of tokens vs. 10.2-46.8%), and query-universal exact merging is provably impossible under RoPE. All thresholds were frozen before data; 20 archived verdicts and 8 closed routes accompany the paper.

</details>


### [170] [OSR: Output Space Redistribution for Adaptive Label Removal in Classification Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03972)
*Minyi Peng,Darian Gunamardi,Ivan Tjuawinata,Yongsen Zheng,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在输出空间中进行统计重分布的方法，用于模拟重训练模型在移除类别标签后的置信度向量，作为一种模块化输出过滤器，规避了特征空间调整或损失函数收敛的负担，在多个分类任务上取得了与完全重训练相当的性能，同时提升了计算效率和隐私保护能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标签体系不断演进的分类系统中，类别的移除频繁发生，模型需要相应调整。现有方法（基于重训练和基于特征空间调整）普遍存在依赖原始数据、计算和存储成本高、结果不一致、扩展性差以及模型效用退化等问题。因此需要一种更高效、可扩展且隐私友好的类别移除方案。

Method: 提出一种在输出空间利用统计重分布来近似重训练模型移除类别后置信度向量的方法。该方法作为模块化输出过滤器发挥作用，无需特征空间调整或损失函数收敛，仅需现有的标签信息和先前的输出置信度即可实现类别移除。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多个分类任务上与完全重训练相比具有竞争力的性能，同时在计算效率和隐私保护方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 输出空间统计重分布方法是一种有效的类别移除方案，在保持与完整重训练可比性能的同时，解决了计算开销、可扩展性和隐私等痛点，为动态分类体系中的标签移除提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Label removal occurs frequently in classification systems with evolving taxonomies, where categories must be dynamically updated or eliminated. To accommodate such changes, classification models must adapt accordingly. Existing solutions, broadly categorized as retraining-based and feature-space-adjustment-based, share common limitations despite their variations, including reliance on access to original data, substantial computational and storage costs, inconsistent results, poor scalability, and degradation of model utility. To address this, we propose a novel approach that leverages statistical redistribution in the output space to approximate the post-removal confidence vectors of a retrained model. Applicable as a modular output filter, our method bypasses the burden of feature-space adjustments or loss-function convergence, alleviating scalability limitations. Furthermore, by requiring only existing labels and prior output confidences, the method potentially mitigates privacy concerns inherent to data-dependent solutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive performance against full retraining, with improvements in computational efficiency and privacy preservation across several classification tasks.

</details>


### [171] [RobustSeiz: An Open-Source Framework for Benchmarking the Robustness of EEG Seizure Detection Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04007)
*Mohammad Mohammadi,Alireza Zarei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RobustSeiz——一个开源、模型无关的框架，用于在受控的、临床动机驱动的分布偏移下对癫痫发作检测器进行压力测试与比较，在部署前统一多个公开数据集并标准化评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癫痫发作检测器在留出数据集上表现良好，但在现实世界的采集变异性、伪影和对抗性输入下可能失效。现有评估缺乏标准化、可复现的压力测试协议，导致部署前无法充分评估模型在临床相关的分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Method: RobustSeiz 将四个公开头皮脑电语料库（CHB-MIT、TUSZ、Siena、SeizeIT1）标准化为 BIDS-EEG 结构，采用受试者无关的划分评估检测器；在预定义超参数网格上扫描环境、噪声和对抗性变换；报告样本级与事件级的灵敏度、精确率、F1、24小时假阳性率、发作起始前后时间误差以及 Monte Carlo dropout 预测一致性；提供 Dockerized GPU 流水线、实验注册表及全评估/研究子集两种模式。

Result: 作者用当代癫痫检测器在 TUSZ 数据集上跨完整实施偏移网格做了验证，并展示 AWGN 分析揭示了扰动严重程度如何改变检测质量、发作起始计时与预测一致性。

Conclusion: RobustSeiz 为在现实临床压力因素下评估癫痫检测器的鲁棒性提供了共享的基准标准，将部署前评估扩展到清洁数据准确率之外。

Abstract: Despite strong performance on held-out electroencephalography (EEG) data, seizure detectors may fail under real-world acquisition variability, artifacts, and adversarial inputs. We introduce RobustSeiz, an open-source, model-agnostic framework that provides a standardized, reproducible protocol for stress-testing and comparing seizure detectors under controlled, clinically motivated distribution shifts before deployment. We standardize four public scalp-EEG corpora (CHB-MIT, TUSZ, Siena, and SeizeIT1) into BIDS-EEG trees and evaluate subject-independent detectors on held-out splits. Environment, noise, and adversarial transforms are swept over predefined hyperparameter grids. Each run reports sample- and event-level sensitivity, precision, F1, false positives per 24 h, Lead and Lag onset timing, and Monte Carlo dropout predictive agreement. RobustSeiz includes a Dockerized GPU pipeline, experiment registry, and full-evaluation and research-subset modes. We demonstrate the framework with a contemporary seizure detector on TUSZ across the complete implemented shift grid; an AWGN analysis illustrates how perturbation severity changes detection quality, onset timing, and predictive agreement. RobustSeiz provides a shared benchmarking standard for evaluating seizure-detector robustness under realistic clinical stressors, extending pre-deployment assessment beyond clean-data accuracy.

</details>


### [172] [Unlocking Lossless Speedups in LLMs via Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04010)
*Subham Sekhar Sahoo,Lingjie Chen,Khiem Pham,Jonathan Geuter,Chaitanya Dwivedi,Varad Pimpalkhute,Yash Akhauri,Alexander Moreno,Mikhail Yurochkin,Zhenting Wang,Mostafa Elhoushi,Nolan Dey,Shane Bergsma,Joel Hestness,John Thickstun,Eric Xing,Zhengzhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出扩散增强LLM（Uno模型），在保持自回归（AR）模型分布不变的同时，利用扩散机制并行生成多个token，通过扩散蒸馏阶段学习轻量级扩散权重，并引入Ψ-Spec采样器实现无损加速和推理时扩展。无需额外草稿模型（区别于投机解码），也无需牺牲底层AR模型质量（区别于扩散LLM）。Uno 8B在智能体工具使用、编程和长上下文推理任务上超越26B DiffusionGemma和Mercury 2，吞吐量超越领先的投机解码方法，较基础AR模型实现最高3倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的成功依赖于next-token prediction，但其自回归结构导致逐token顺序生成的低效率瓶颈。现有加速方法（如投机解码需额外草稿模型，扩散LLM会牺牲生成质量）均存在不足，希望设计一种既能并行生成token又能保持AR模型质量的架构。

Method: 提出diffusion-augmented LLMs，将模型参数解耦为AR权重和轻量扩散权重两部分：AR权重用标准NTP目标训练，扩散权重通过简单的Diffusion Distillation阶段学习以并行生成多个token。同时提出Ψ-Spec采样器族，支持无损加速和固定上下文长度下的推理时扩展。模型可从零训练或通过增强现有开源权重AR LLM构建。

Result: Uno模型在所有评估的batch size下吞吐量均超越领先的投机解码方法；相对基础AR模型实现最高3倍速度提升（包括设备支持的最大batch size）。8B Uno模型在智能体工具使用、编程和长上下文推理的所有评估基准上超越26B DiffusionGemma及专有的Mercury 2模型。

Conclusion: Uno证明了扩散机制可以与AR模型目标完美结合：既能保留AR模型的质量分布，又能通过并行生成显著加速推理。架构轻量（扩散权重开销可忽略），支持从零训练或增强现有模型，在速度和模型质量上均优于现有投机解码和扩散LLM方案，为该领域提供了实用性强的加速新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) owe much of their success to next-token prediction (NTP), but their autoregressive (AR) structure requires slow, sequential token generation. To overcome this bottleneck, we introduce diffusion-augmented LLMs, a new class of models that defines an AR model distribution while using diffusion to draw multiple tokens in parallel from that distribution. We decouple the parameters of these models into two sets: AR weights, trained using the standard NTP objective, and lightweight diffusion weights, trained to generate multiple tokens simultaneously. The diffusion weights are learned through a simple Diffusion Distillation phase that adds negligible overhead to existing LLM training pipelines. We also introduce $Ψ$-Spec, a family of samplers that enables lossless acceleration and inference-time scaling at a fixed context length. Unlike speculative decoding, our method requires no separate draft model. Unlike diffusion LLMs (d-LLMs), it accelerates generation without sacrificing the quality of the underlying AR model. The resulting models, called Uno, can be trained from scratch or built by augmenting existing open-weight AR LLMs. Uno achieves higher throughput than leading speculative-decoding methods at every evaluated batch size and delivers up to $3\times$ speedups over the base AR model, including at the largest batch size supported by the device. Notably, our 8B Uno model outperforms the leading open d-LLM, the 26B DiffusionGemma, and the proprietary Mercury 2 across all evaluated benchmarks in agentic tool use, coding, and long-context reasoning. We release code and checkpoints at: https://s-sahoo.github.io/uno/

</details>


### [173] [A location-invariant estimator of extremal quantile treatment effects for heavy-tailed distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04018)
*Xin Yu,Shuwei Huang,Jicheng Liu,Jielin Tang,Bolin Wang,Yunxiao Zhang,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对重尾潜在结果下极值分位数处理效应(QTE)估计量对共同位置平移不敏感(invariance)的问题，提出了两步解决方案：将Fraga极值指数估计器适配到因果设定（基于逆倾向得分加权），并用差分式外推方案替代原始公式使位置参数抵消，从而得到位置不变的极值QTE估计量，并建立了相合性、渐近正态性和一致方差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极值分位数水平下的QTE估计在应用中至关重要，但现有基于外推与因果极值指数(EVI)的估计量对潜在结果分布的共同位置平移不具不变性，尽管总体QTE本身是位置不变的。这一不一致性导致估计结果在数据经历位置平移时会失真。

Method: 两步法：(1) 将Fraga EVI估计器通过逆倾向得分加权适配到因果框架，实现位置不变性；(2) 以差分式外推方案替代原始四分位数外推公式，取分位数差时位置参数自然抵消。随后建立估计量的相合性和渐近正态性，并提供一致方差估计量。

Result: 通过模拟研究验证了所提出方法的三个关键性质：位置不变性、对阈值的稳定性以及置信区间的覆盖率；理论结果给出了估计量的相合性与渐近正态性。

Conclusion: 所提出的位置不变的极值QTE估计量在理论和数值上均表现良好，可以在极值分位水平下提供渐近有效的统计推断，克服了既有估计器在位置平移下的缺陷。

Abstract: Quantile treatment effects (QTEs) measure the effect of a treatment on the distribution of an outcome, and their estimation at extreme quantile levels is of central interest in applications where the target quantiles lie far beyond the range of the data. For heavy-tailed potential outcomes, existing extremal QTE estimators rely on extrapolation combined with a causal extreme value index (EVI) estimator, but the resulting estimator is not invariant under a common location shift of the potential outcome distributions, even though the population QTE is. We address this issue in two steps. First, we adapt the location-invariant Fraga estimator of the EVI to the causal setting using inverse propensity score weighting. Second, we replace the original extrapolation formula with a difference-based scheme, under which the location parameter cancels when quantile differences are taken. The resulting QTE estimator is therefore location invariant. We establish the consistency and asymptotic normality of the proposed extremal QTE estimators, and provide a consistent variance estimator, leading to asymptotically valid inference. A simulation study confirms the location invariance, the stability with respect to the threshold, and the coverage of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [174] [Subspace Inference Enables Efficient Active Reward Learning from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04066)
*Yutai Zhou,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PreferenceEKF，一种将主动偏好学习建模为序列贝叶斯滤波问题的方法。它通过扩展卡尔曼滤波器在低维参数子空间中进行序贯推断，避免了在全神经网络参数空间上不可行的后验推断，从而高效追踪奖励模型的不确定性并计算采集函数。在D4RL和V-D4RL基准上，该方法在样本效率、运行时间、可扩展性和校准方面优于其他贝叶斯深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF从人类偏好中学习奖励模型虽然强大但样本效率低下，因此主动学习是合成有信息量偏好查询的关键。然而，对大型神经网络奖励模型进行有效的不确定性量化是主动学习面临的核心挑战。现有的全参数空间后验推断方法计算代价过高。

Method: 将主动偏好学习形式化为序列贝叶斯滤波问题，在低维参数子空间中使用扩展卡尔曼滤波器进行序贯推断，随着新的偏好查询到来不断更新奖励模型的后验。该方法能够从神经网络参数中进行可扩展地采样，以高效计算主动奖励学习的采集函数。

Result: 在D4RL和V-D4RL基准上，PreferenceEKF在样本效率、运行时间、可扩展性和校准方面均优于其他贝叶斯深度学习方法，且学到的奖励模型可带来具有竞争力的离线强化学习策略性能。

Conclusion: PreferenceEKF展示了可扩展贝叶斯方法在RLHF中基于偏好的奖励建模领域的潜力，为大型神经网络奖励模型的不确定性量化提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a powerful yet sample-inefficient approach for learning reward models from human preferences, making active learning a critical component in synthesizing informative preference queries. However, effective uncertainty quantification required for active learning remains a key challenge for large neural network reward models. In this paper, we introduce PreferenceEKF, a sample-efficient approach that tracks reward model uncertainty by framing active preference learning as a sequential Bayesian filtering problem. Instead of relying on computationally prohibitive posterior inference over the full neural network parameter space, our method performs sequential inference via an extended Kalman filter within a low-dimensional parameter subspace, continuously updating the reward model posterior as new preference queries arrive. Our approach enables scalable sampling of neural network parameters to efficiently compute acquisition functions for active reward learning. Experiments on the D4RL and V-D4RL benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves better sample efficiency, runtime, scalability, and calibration compared to other Bayesian deep learning approaches, and the learned reward models lead to competitive offline reinforcement learning policy performance. This highlights the potential of scalable Bayesian methods for preference-based reward modeling in RLHF. Our code is available at https://github.com/yutaizhou/bnn_pref.

</details>


### [175] [Conditioning Degenerate Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04090)
*Uğur Aydın,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用因果最优传输（causal optimal transport）来定义近似损失函数，用于在扩散系数奇异且条件密度不存在或不光滑的情况下，识别最小熵控制的引导信号，作为传统基于分数函数（score function）引导方法的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前条件生成模型严重依赖分数函数进行训练引导，但当生成模型是扩散系数奇异的扩散过程，且底层条件密度不存在或非光滑时，传统基于分数的引导方法失效。作者旨在提供一种在极少量假设下仍可工作的引导方法。

Method: 利用因果最优传输及其通过（条件）扩散过程的可测表示性质（predictable representation property）的表征来定义近似损失函数，其中相关鞅问题适定（well posed），借鉴Üstünel的理论框架。通过最小熵控制实现对生成过程的最优引导。

Result: 该方法在最小假设条件下即可识别最小熵控制用于引导，克服了条件密度不存在或不光滑以及扩散系数奇异所带来的困难，为条件生成模型提供了一种理论上稳健的引导机制。

Conclusion: 因果最优传输为奇异扩散条件下的条件生成提供了一条不依赖于分数函数光滑性和密度存在的替代路径，扩展了条件扩散模型引导方法的应用范围与理论保证。

Abstract: Current conditioned generative models heavily rely on score functions for guidance during training. When the generative model is a diffusion process with a singular diffusion coefficient and the underlying (conditional) densities either do not exist or are not smooth, we use causal optimal transport to define \emph{approximate} loss functions that identify a minimum-entropy control for guidance under minimal assumptions. Our approach relies on causal optimal transport and its characterization through the predictable representation property of (conditioned) diffusion processes whose associated martingale problem is well posed, à la Üstünel.

</details>


### [176] [Hardware-Aware FP4 FlashAttention-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04105)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Direct-P方法，解决Blackwell 4位浮点（FP4）张量核心在注意力计算中因softmax转换和片上依赖导致的性能瓶颈。Direct-P将注意力分数直接映射为FP4概率，在非因果推理中相对BF16前向吞吐量最高提升2.13倍；同时提出因果路径将前向量化直接传入反向传播，通过重构概率并使用FP8梯度操作数，使单GPU 80亿参数完整更新最高加速1.14倍。匹配的分布式训练保留FP8概率和值，所有测试的MXFP4概率/值训练轨迹均发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blackwell的FP4张量核心虽然提供了低精度计算能力，但在注意力机制中并不能自动加速，因为softmax转换和片上依赖在矩阵乘积缩小时成为主导开销。作者旨在消除这些瓶颈，使FP4精度能够真正提升注意力及端到端训练/推理性能。

Method: 提出Direct-P方法：非因果推理路径将注意力分数直接映射为FP4概率，避免softmax的格式转换开销；因果路径则将前向量化直接传入反向传播，并从保存的量化查询与键中重构概率，同时使用FP8梯度操作数进行计算。

Result: 在NVIDIA GB200上，非因果推理前向吞吐量相对BF16最高提升2.13倍；完整单GPU 80亿参数更新最高加速1.14倍。分布式训练中，匹配的FP8精度下概率和值均保持稳定，而所有测试的MXFP4概率/值训练轨迹均发散（无法收敛）。

Conclusion: Direct-P有效消除了FP4注意力中的softmax与依赖瓶颈，在推理和前向/更新阶段均取得显著加速。但结果表明，在MXFP4精度下进行训练会导致发散，因此训练场景需要保留FP8精度，仅推理阶段可安全采用FP4。

Abstract: Blackwell's 4-bit floating-point (FP4) tensor cores do not automatically make attention faster because softmax conversion and on-chip dependencies dominate once its matrix products shrink. We address this with \emph{Direct-P} for noncausal inference and a causal path that passes the forward quantization directly into backward. Direct-P maps scores directly to FP4 probabilities and reaches up to 2.13$\times$ the bfloat16 (BF16) forward throughput on an NVIDIA GB200. The causal path reconstructs probabilities from saved quantized queries and keys and uses 8-bit floating-point (FP8) gradient operands, accelerating a complete single-GPU 8-billion-parameter update by up to 1.14$\times$. Matched distributed training retains FP8 probabilities and values; every tested MXFP4 probability/value training trajectory diverges.

</details>


### [177] [Constant regret in general games via higher-order optimism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04113)
*Omar Abbadi,Rida Laraki,Panayotis Mertikopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HOOD算法（高阶乐观折扣算法），一种非耦合学习算法，用于任意N人正规型博弈（每人最多K个动作），保证每个玩家的个体遗憾为O(N³log²K)，且在整个博弈时间范围内均匀成立。该算法结合了带折扣的(N+1)阶预测器与熵正则化，通过对博弈策略空间的"提升"处理来设计，旨在可控地抑制博弈序列的大幅振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在通用博弈中实现常数遗憾（与时间无关的遗憾界）一直是一个困难问题。先前尝试的一个关键障碍是诱导的博弈序列存在大幅振荡。作者希望通过高阶乐观预测与折扣机制的组合，解决这一振荡问题，从而在一般N人博弈中实现与时间无关的常数遗憾界。

Method: 提出的HOOD算法是乐观跟随正则化领导者（OptFTRL）的一个变体。核心方法包括三个关键成分：(1) 使用带折扣的(N+1)阶预测器；(2) 在博弈策略空间的一个合适"提升"（lifting）上施加熵正则化；(3) 这些组合被专门设计用于以可控方式抑制诱导博弈序列中的大幅振荡。该算法是非耦合的（uncoupled），即每个玩家仅根据自己的信息行动。

Result: 当所有玩家都使用HOOD算法时，对于任意N人正规型博弈（每人最多K个动作），每个玩家的个体遗憾为O(N³log²K)，该界在整个博弈时间范围内均匀成立（即与时间T无关，属于常数遗憾界）。

Conclusion: 该工作通过高阶乐观与折扣的巧妙结合，消除了通用博弈中实现常数遗憾的主要障碍。作者还指出，他们与Liu, Farina和Ozdaglar的独立并发工作（该工作通过高阶乐观与指数移动平均估计器获得O(N²¹log⁴K)界）存在显著相似性，但本文获得的界显著更优。

Abstract: We introduce an uncoupled learning algorithm which, when employed by all players of an arbitrary $N$-player normal form game with up to $K$ actions per player, guarantees $O(N^3\log^2 K)$ individual regret, uniformly over the horizon of play. The proposed algorithm - which we call higher-order optimism with discounting (HOOD) is a variant of optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader (OptFTRL) that combines a discounted $(N+1)$-th order predictor with entropic regularization over a suitable "lifting" of the game's strategy space. This combination of ingredients is purposefully designed to dampen large oscillations of the induced sequence of play in a controlled manner, removing in this way a key stumbling block of previous attempts to achieve constant regret in general games. Our approach bears several striking similarities to the concurrent - and completely independent - work of Liu, Farina, and Ozdaglar (arXiv:2608.31166), who very recently derived an $O(N^{21}\log^{4} K)$ regret bound through the use of higher-order optimism and an exponential moving average estimator.

</details>


### [178] [Prospective Coding Improves Learning in Deep Continuous-Time Recurrent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04134)
*Shivang Rawat,Mirko Morello,Flaviano Morone,David J. Heeger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归正交滤波器（RQFs），一种由生物学启发的复值时间滤波器（对角状态空间模型的特殊情况），并引入"前瞻输入"（prospective-input）机制来缓解深层连续时间循环网络中因时间积分导致的底部信号延迟与顶部误差衰减问题，在完整BPTT和仅空间反向传播下均显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间循环网络通过时间积分获得记忆，但在深层堆叠中，时间积分会延迟底部上行信号并衰减顶部下传误差，导致深层网络的梯度衰减和训练困难。作者希望从生物学角度出发，通过让每一层的底部输入"前瞻化"来解决这一失效模式。

Method: 从能量模型出发推导RQF动力学，证明每个RQF是带通滤波器，可学习参数控制其调谐频率和带宽。通过参数自由的二抽头（two-tap）更新使每层的底部输入前瞻化，该更新不改变递推转移和并行扫描结构。将该纠正方法推广至一般对角SSM，并验证其对仅空间反向传播造成梯度截断时的深度相关梯度衰减有缓解作用。在RQFs、S5和ORGaNICs三种模型上分别用完整BPTT和仅空间反向传播进行训练评估。

Result: 在完整BPTT下，前瞻变体在所有模型和配置中均匹配或超越非前瞻对照。非残差宽度32六层RQF在raw-audio Speech Commands上以31.9k参数达到96.09%准确率；宽度64的六层RQF在16,384步Path-X任务上达到83.56%。

Conclusion: RQFs被证明是一种参数高效的循环处理基元，而前瞻输入编码是面向深度连续时间循环网络的一种输入侧纠正方案，有效缓解了深度依赖的梯度衰减问题。

Abstract: Temporal integration gives continuous-time recurrent networks memory, but in deep stacks it also delays bottom-up signals and attenuates top-down errors. We develop Recursive Quadrature Filters (RQFs), biologically motivated complex-valued temporal filters that are a special case of diagonal state-space models (SSMs), and ask whether this failure mode can be addressed by making each layer's bottom-up input prospective. Starting from an energy model, we derive the RQF dynamics and show that each RQF is a band-pass filter whose learnable parameters control its tuning frequency and bandwidth. We then make each layer's bottom-up input prospective using a parameter-free two-tap update that leaves the recurrent transition and parallel scan unchanged. We extend this correction to general diagonal SSMs and show that it mitigates depth-dependent gradient attenuation when temporal gradients are truncated, i.e., spatial-only backpropagation. We evaluate the intervention in RQFs, S5, and ORGaNICs (a nonlinear gated RNN) trained using full backpropagation through time (BPTT) and spatial-only backpropagation. Under full BPTT, prospective variants match or outperform their non-prospective controls in every model and configuration. A non-residual width-32 six-layer RQF reaches 96.09% accuracy on raw-audio Speech Commands with 31.9k parameters; a width-64 six-layer RQF reaches 83.56% on the 16,384-step Path-X task. These results identify RQFs as a parameter-efficient recurrent substrate and prospective-input coding as an input-side correction for deep continuous-time recurrent networks.

</details>


### [179] [A Low-Cost, Open Platform for End-to-End Autonomous Driving on a Miniature Ackermann Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04147)
*Gustavo Claudio Karl Couto,Eric Aislan Antonelo,Gabriel George Zipperer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个低成本、开源的小型Ackermann车辆端到端自动驾驶实验平台，结合实体车辆、印刷城市轨道、数据采集、轨迹配准和Webots数字孪生，用于连接仿真与真实世界的自动驾驶研究。实验表明，命令条件行为克隆策略在实体车上平均横向误差6.1cm，接近人类演示的4.7cm；在数字孪生中摄像头视场从58°增至120°时误差从35.6cm降至3.3cm。融合合成数据与真实演示训练的更高容量策略是唯一能闭环完成全部四条赛道轨迹的配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶研究在仿真与真实世界之间存在显著差距，缺乏低成本、可控且可复现的实验平台来连接两者，导致sim-to-real方法难以进行系统性验证。

Method: 构建一个包含物理车辆、印刷轨道、数据采集工具、轨迹配准和Webots数字孪生的开源平台；实现命令条件行为克隆基线（输入车载摄像头图像与高层导航命令，输出转向和速度）；引入可学习的sim-to-real图像转换器以缩小外观差距，结合合成数据与真实演示训练更高容量策略。

Result: 真实闭环实验中，学习策略平均横向误差6.1cm，接近人类演示的4.7cm；数字孪生中摄像头视场从58°拓宽到120°将误差从35.6cm降至3.3cm；结合合成数据与真实演示训练的高容量策略是唯一能在闭环中完成全部四条赛道轨迹的配置，而紧凑基线和仅用真实数据训练的同类网络完成更少。

Conclusion: 该开源平台可作为实用的sim-to-real研究测试平台，为命令条件模仿学习提供了初步基线，并支持可复现研究。

Abstract: This paper presents a low-cost, open experimental platform for research in end-to-end autonomous driving with miniature Ackermann vehicles. The platform combines a physical vehicle, a printed urban track, data collection tools, trajectory registration, and a Webots digital twin, enabling controlled experiments that connect simulation-based autonomous-driving methods to real-world execution. As a first baseline, we implement command-conditioned behavior cloning, in which a neural policy receives an on-board camera image and a high-level navigation command and outputs steering and speed. The system is evaluated both on the physical vehicle and in simulation. In real closed-loop experiments, the learned policy follows lanes and executes commanded turns, reaching a mean cross-track error of 6.1 cm with respect to the reference route, close to the 4.7 cm observed in human demonstrations. In the digital twin, camera field of view has a strong effect on performance, reducing the mean cross-track error from 35.6 to 3.3 cm when widened from 58 to 120 degrees. Using the digital twin to generate synthetic driving data and a learned sim-to-real image translator to reduce the appearance gap, we further show that a higher-capacity policy trained on this synthetic data combined with real demonstrations is the only configuration that completes all four track routes in closed loop, whereas the compact baseline and the same network trained on real data alone complete fewer. These results establish the open platform as a practical testbed for sim-to-real studies and provide an initial command-conditioned imitation-learning baseline; we release it to support reproducible research.

</details>


### [180] [Robust PAC Learning of Concurrent Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04189)
*Angel Y. He,David Parker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个用于一般和并发随机博弈（CSG）的PAC学习框架，该框架在转移不确定性下同时处理纳什均衡存在性的挑战，通过在L1置信集上求解鲁棒CSG来计算社会福利最优的ε-近似NE，并引入Nash margin特征来证明均衡存在性或提供不存在精确NE的证书，样本复杂度为多项式级别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一般和并发随机博弈中，转移不确定性下的学习问题长期缺乏形式化的理论保证，且纳什均衡的存在性问题（不像单目标MDP/矩阵博弈那样总有解）一直是阻碍在CSG中应用强化学习框架的核心障碍。现有研究要么回避NE存在性问题，要么需要较强的假设，缺乏一个统一的PAC学习框架。

Method: 算法核心包括三个组件：(1) 数据驱动的L1置信集构造，用于刻画转移核的不确定性；(2) 基于鲁棒MDP的探索机制驱动联合状态-动作覆盖，通过求解鲁棒CSG计算社会福利最优的ε-近似NE；(3) 新颖的Nash margin表征，用于对均衡存在性进行原则性推理——若存在则可返回ε-近似NE且社会福利ε-接近最优，否则提供无精确NE存在的可靠证书。

Result: 在最小可达性条件p_reach>0下，算法经多项式数量的轨迹样本终止，样本复杂度为Õ(R_max²H⁴|S|²|A|/(p_reach·ε²))。基准CSG上的实证结果表明算法达到近最优性能，正确处理均衡（不）存在性，且样本复杂度与理论一致。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了CSG中学习与博弈论分析之间的鸿沟：通过PAC框架和Nash margin特征，使得在转移不确定性下既能学习到近似NE（当存在时），又能对NE不存在的情形给出程序化证书，为一般和CSG提供了首个端到端可保证的学习算法。

Abstract: We introduce the first Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learning framework for general-sum concurrent stochastic games (CSGs) with transition uncertainty, while addressing the challenge of Nash equilibrium (NE) existence. Our algorithm maintains data-driven $L^1$ confidence sets over transition kernels and solves a robust CSG to compute a social-welfare optimal $\varepsilon$-NE, using a robust MDP-based exploration mechanism to drive joint state-action coverage. Crucially, we introduce a Nash margin characterisation that enables principled reasoning about equilibrium existence: the framework either returns an $\varepsilon$-approximate NE whose social-welfare value is $\varepsilon$-close to optimal, or provides a sound certificate that no exact NE exists. Under a minimum reachability condition $p_{\mathrm{reach}}>0$ over relevant state-action pairs, the algorithm terminates after a polynomial number of trajectory samples, with sample complexity $\widetilde{O}\left( {R_{\max}^2 H^4 |S|^2 |A| / (p_{\mathrm{reach}} \varepsilon^2)} \right)$. Empirical results on benchmark CSGs demonstrate near-optimal performance, correct handling of equilibrium (non-)existence, and sample complexity consistent with theory.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Occupancy-based Quantile Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03104)
*Zihao Shi,Huajun Xi,Bingyi Jing,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于占用率的分位数风险控制（OQRC）方法，通过将风险控制建模为有限占用率问题，在保障有限样本有效性的同时提供紧密的风险控制界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分位数风险控制方法要么过于保守（导致资源浪费），要么缺乏严格的有限样本保证。为克服这些局限，需要一种既能提供紧密风险上界又具备严格有限样本有效性的新方法。

Method: 将风险控制问题转化为有限占用率问题，用有序校准损失对损失空间进行分箱；估计测试损失在各箱中的分布，以每箱内最大损失作为风险上界；选择满足特定概率条件的参数λ，使该上界以高概率1-δ不超过预定阈值α。

Result: 理论上建立了有限样本保证，证明OQRC的风险控制界限以O_p(n^{-1/2})的速度收敛到最优界限；实验结果表明该方法在常用基准上将风险差距最多降低了78.64%。

Conclusion: OQRC提供了一种兼具有限样本有效性与紧密性（非保守）的分位数风险控制方案，在理论上有快速收敛保证，并在实践中显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Conformal risk control is an emerging framework for the safe deployment of machine learning models with finite-sample guarantees. To accommodate a broader class of risk notions, quantile risk control extends this framework to quantile-based risk measures. However, existing methods either suffer from excessive conservatism or lack rigorous finite-sample guarantees. To address these limitations, we introduce Occupancy-based Quantile Risk Control (OQRC), a novel method that provides tight risk control bounds with finite-sample validity. Our key idea is to formulate risk control as a finite-occupancy problem by partitioning the loss space with the ordered calibration losses. Specifically, we estimate the distribution of test losses across the resulting bins and upper-bound the risk by the maximum loss attained within each bin. We then select the parameter $λ$ such that this upper bound does not exceed a predefined threshold $α$ with high probability $1-δ$. Theoretically, we establish a finite-sample guarantee showing that OQRC yields tight risk control bounds that converge to the optimal bounds at a provable rate of $\mathcal{O}_ p(n^{-1/2})$. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, reducing the risk gap by up to 78.64\% on common benchmarks.

</details>


### [182] [A Closed-Form Formula for Consistent Lipschitz Regression on Metric Spaces with Sparse Neural Network Realizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03129)
*Ruiyang Hong,Hrad Ghoukasian,Anastasis Kratsios*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于从有噪观测中重建未知Lipschitz函数的简单闭式"两阶段"组合公式，具备统一的L∞恢复保证（零优化误差），并在函数空间维度、参数空间数值稳定性、前向正则性三个互补意义上证明其最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的机器学习方法（如KRR、SVR）因具有闭式解或凸优化目标而兼具计算与解析上的可处理性，而深度神经网络通常不具备这些特性。作者希望引入一种既保留深度模型表达能力、又具有闭式解析结构的方法来重建度量空间上的未知Lipschitz函数。

Method: 提出一个简单的闭式"两阶段"组合公式 f̂，用于在有噪数据下重建度量空间 (X,ρ) 上的未知Lipschitz函数。分析方法上联合控制逼近误差与统计误差，且优化误差为零（不依赖近似ERM的oracle）。当 X=[0,1]^d 配以 ℓ∞ 范数时，该公式可用深度 O(log N)、参数 O(N) 的ReLU-MLP和精确ReLU多头transformer实现。

Result: 主要结果为高概率均匀（L∞）恢复保证；在Ahlfors正则度量空间上，该假设类达到最优fat-shattering维数；参数依赖达到最大数值稳定性；输入依赖达到与目标函数Lipschitz常数匹配的最大正则性。

Conclusion: 作者从函数空间表达能力、参数空间数值稳定性和前向传播正则性三个维度建立了所提公式的最优性，同时证明了其在常见设置下可分别用ReLU-MLP和ReLU多头transformer高效实现，兼顾了理论可处理性与深度模型的表达能力。

Abstract: Several classical machine-learning methods, such as KRRs and SVRs, are both computationally and analytically tractable since their estimators either admit closed-form expressions or are obtained by minimizing convex training objectives; neither feature is generally available for deep neural networks. We address this by introducing a simple closed-form ``two-stage'' compositional formula $\hat{f}$ for reconstructing an unknown Lipschitz function $f:\mathcal{X}\to \mathbb{R}$ on a metric space $(\mathcal X,ρ)$ from $N$ i.i.d. noisy observations.
  Our main result is a high-probability uniform ($L^{\infty}$) recovery guarantee that jointly controls approximation and statistical errors while enjoying an optimization error of zero; in particular, we do not assume oracle access to an approximate ERM. Our secondary main results establish the optimality of our formula in three complementary senses. 1) Function space: On Ahlfors-regular metric spaces, the hypothesis class parameterized by our formula attains the optimal fat-shattering dimension. 2) Parameter space: Its dependence on the parameters is maximally numerically stable, in the sense that a smaller approximation error cannot be achieved with a smaller Lipschitz dependence on the model parameters. 3) Forward pass: Its dependence on the input is maximally regular, matching the Lipschitz constant of the target function $f$. When $\mathcal X=[0,1]^d$ is equipped with the $\ell^\infty$ norm, $\hat{f}$ admits algorithmic ReLU-MLP and exact ReLU-multi-head transformer realizations of depth $\mathcal{O}(\log(N))$ with $\mathcal{O}(N)$ nonzero parameters.

</details>


### [183] [ALRA: Adaptive Local Relational Alignment for Logit-Based Pre-training Distillation of Autoregressive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03355)
*Quang Hoang Trung,Quang Huu Hieu,Nguyen Van Hoang Phuc,Vo Nguyen Le Duy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应局部关系对齐（ALRA），一种用于自回归语言模型logit知识蒸馏的位置特异性框架，结合学生候选与教师锚定token，通过自适应局部散度和学生加权成对关系对齐来实现更精确的分布匹配。在Pile数据集上以随机初始化的200M和500M学生模型测试，ALRA分别超出最强蒸馏基线0.94和0.83个百分点，并较无蒸馏预训练提升2.31和2.91个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的基于logit的知识蒸馏方法采用全局目标对齐教师与学生的全词表next-token分布，忽视了候选token之间的相对偏好；而现有局部方法仅从教师或学生单一侧选择候选token——教师单独选择会遗漏学生认为可能的token，学生单独选择则在训练早期依赖不准确的排序。因此需要一种能同时结合教师与学生信息的自适应、位置特异的局部对齐框架。

Method: ALRA框架在每个有效预测位置结合学生提议的likely tokens与教师最高概率token作为锚点。根据教师在当前候选集中概率分布的集中度（相对于当前批次），动态调整所选token数量。包含两个核心组件：(1) Adaptive Local Divergence——保留mass-matching项，并将候选集与剩余词表区域的相对分布分别对齐；与精确的全词表分解不同，它将两个条件项的教师mass系数替换为单位系数，避免因区域教师概率低而被过度降权。(2) Student-Weighted Pairwise Relational Alignment——强调具有小概率差距的高概率token对，对低概率或明显分离的token对给予更低权重。

Result: 在The Pile上以随机初始化的200M和500M参数学生模型跨九个zero-shot基准测试，平均准确率分别达36.62%和37.40%。ALRA较最强蒸馏基线分别提升0.94和0.83个百分点，较无语蒸馏的预训练分别提升2.31和2.91个百分点。

Conclusion: ALRA通过自适应的局部token选择与改进的相对分布对齐策略，有效解决了自回归语言模型蒸馏中全词表目标忽视相对偏好、以及单侧候选选择不全面的问题，证明将学生提议与教师引导相结合的局部关系对齐能显著提升蒸馏质量。

Abstract: Logit-based knowledge distillation for autoregressive language models usually aligns teacher and student next-token distributions over the entire vocabulary. However, this global objective overlooks relative preferences among likely token alternatives. Existing local approaches often select candidate tokens from either the teacher or the student alone. Teacher-only selection can miss tokens that the student considers likely, while student-only selection can rely on an inaccurate ranking early in training. We propose Adaptive Local Relational Alignment (ALRA), a position-specific framework combining student proposals with teacher guidance. At each valid prediction position, the student proposes likely tokens, while the teacher's most probable token is included as an anchor. ALRA adjusts the number of selected tokens according to how broadly the teacher distributes probability within this candidate set relative to the current batch. Adaptive Local Divergence retains the mass-matching term and separately matches the relative token distributions within the selected and remaining vocabulary regions. Unlike the exact full-vocabulary decomposition, it replaces the teacher-mass coefficients of the two conditional terms with unit coefficients, preventing either term from being downweighted solely because its region has low teacher probability. Student-Weighted Pairwise Relational Alignment emphasizes high-probability token pairs with small student probability gaps and gives less weight to unlikely or clearly separated pairs. Experiments on The Pile with randomly initialized 200M- and 500M-parameter students across nine zero-shot benchmarks yield average accuracies of 36.62% and 37.40%. ALRA exceeds the strongest competing distillation baseline by 0.94 and 0.83 percentage points and improves over pre-training without distillation by 2.31 and 2.91 points, respectively.

</details>


### [184] [Towards a Statistical Understanding of Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03501)
*Siyuan He,Bokai Yang,Jie Hu,Ziwen Gao,Yuhong Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从"局部化聚合"视角统一理解混合专家（MoE）架构，推导了学习密集与稀疏路由、含共享专家时的Oracle风险界，分离了逼近误差、专家学习误差与路由估计误差，并刻画了稀疏Top-K路由如何在保持局部化聚合优势的同时控制每输入计算量，最终建立了理解MoE专家专业化与计算权衡的统一统计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构在现代大规模模型中日益重要，但其设计选择（路由机制、稀疏激活、共享专家）的统计作用尚不完全清楚。现有理论大多聚焦于参数化或正确设定的MoE模型，未能揭示这些设计背后的统计本质，因此需要一个新的理论视角来弥合理论与实践之间的鸿沟。

Method: 将MoE视为一种局部化聚合（localized aggregation）形式，从这一视角重新审视逼近-估计-计算三者间的权衡。具体包括：(1) 为学习密集和稀疏路由（含动态演化的专家）推导Oracle风险界，并将误差分解为逼近误差、专家学习误差和路由估计误差三部分；(2) 刻画稀疏Top-K路由如何在保留局部聚合收益的同时降低每输入计算量；(3) 通过输入空间的几何性质解读门控机制，将路由性能与局部专家优势区域联系起来；(4) 分析如DeepSeekMoE架构中共享专家的作用，说明其如何提取公共预测结构，使路由专家专注于残差局部变化。

Result: 得到了分离逼近、专家学习与路由估计三类误差的风险界；证明了稀疏Top-K路由能够以可控的每输入计算量保留局部化聚合的优势；揭示了路由性能与输入空间中局部专家优势区域的几何关联；阐释了共享专家提取公共结构、路由专家专注局部残差变化的机制。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个统一的统计框架，将MoE理解为输入依赖的专家聚合：专家专业化程度与计算开销均由局部预测结构所支配。该框架统一解释了MoE中路由、稀疏激活与共享专家等设计选择背后的统计原理。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures increase model capacity by combining a collection of expert predictors through input-dependent routing, while often activating only a small subset of experts for each input. Despite their growing importance in modern large-scale models, the statistical roles of their design choices, especially routing, sparse activation, and shared experts, remain only partially understood, as existing theory has largely focused on parametric or correctly specified MoE models. In this paper, we view MoE as a form of localized aggregation and show how this localization reshapes the approximation-estimation-computation tradeoff. We derive oracle risk bounds for learning dense and sparse routing with evolving experts, separating approximation, expert-learning, and router-estimation errors, and characterize how sparse Top-K routing can retain the benefits of localized aggregation while controlling per-input computation. We also interpret gating through the geometry of input space, relating routing performance to regions of local expert advantage, and show how shared experts, as adopted in architectures such as DeepSeekMoE, can extract common predictive structure so that routed experts focus on residual local variation. Together, these results provide a unified statistical framework for understanding MoE through input-dependent expert aggregation, in which expert specialization and computational tradeoffs are governed by local predictive structure.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Artin-Schreier geproci configurations in projective spaces of arbitrary dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03024)
*Luca Chiantini,Lucja Farnik,Giuseppe Favacchio,Brian Harbourne,Juan Migliore,Tomasz Szemberg,Justyna Szpond*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入\mathbb{F}_N-Artin-Schreier构造，在任意射影维数和任意正特征下构建了有限geproci集。在\mathbb{P}^3中完整刻画了此类构造何时为geproci的条件，并通过提升构造在任意维数\mathbb{P}^n中产生geproci集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: geproci集（generalized projected complete intersection）是射影几何中一类重要的构造对象，此前的研究主要集中在低维情形。作者希望突破维数和特征的限制，在更一般的射影空间和正特征环境下构造geproci集。

Method: 引入\mathbb{F}_N-Artin-Schreier配置这一新的构造工具；在\mathbb{P}^3中对这类配置进行完整的geproci性质刻画（证明当且仅当q≤N时配置为(q,N)-geproci）；进一步发展一种提升（lifting）构造方法，将\mathbb{P}^3中的结果提升到\mathbb{P}^n（n≥3）。

Result: （1）在任意射影维数和任意正特征下均能构造出有限的geproci集；（2）在\mathbb{P}^3中，\mathbb{F}_N-Artin-Schreier配置在q条直线上的geproci条件被精确刻画为q≤N；（3）通过提升构造，成功在\mathbb{P}^n（n≥3）中生成了新的geproci集。

Conclusion: 该工作将geproci集的构造推广到了任意射影维数及任意正特征，建立了\mathbb{P}^3中一个清晰且完整的判定准则，并给出了高维构造的有效方法，显著拓展了geproci集理论的研究范围和适用场景。

Abstract: We construct finite geproci sets in every projective dimension and in every positive characteristic by introducing $\mathbb{F}_N$-Artin-Schreier configurations. In $\mathbb{P}^3$, we characterize exactly when such configurations are geproci: an $\mathbb{F}_N$-Artin-Schreier configuration on $q$ lines spanning $\mathbb{P}^3$ is $(q,N)$-geproci if and only if $q\leq N$. We then develop a lifting construction which produces geproci sets in $\mathbb{P}^n$ for every $n\geq 3$.

</details>


### [186] [Equilibria for Networks of Linear Translational Springs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03143)
*Luke Oeding,Ethan Clayton,Jackson Elsea,Nicholas Wang,Adam Rutkowski*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文运用非线性代数工具（同伦延拓、monodromy、参数同伦等方法）研究了小型线性平移弹簧网络的平衡点问题，求解了2维和3维中至多5个节点的所有刚性线性平移弹簧网络，并给出了平面弹簧网络最大解数的精确总次数界，同时对比了多种方法的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹簧网络的平衡点求解是结构力学和系统生物学等领域中的基本问题，通常需要求解非线性多项式方程组。作者希望通过非线性代数的数值方法系统地研究小规模弹簧网络的平衡构型数量与结构，弥补传统数值方法在这类问题上的不足。

Method: 本文采用非线性代数的求解技术，包括同伦延拓（homotopy continuation）、monodromy方法和参数同伦（cheater homotopy）。其中参数同伦的实现方法由系统的物理结构自然导出。此外还结合了多面体同伦方法（polyhedral homotopy）以进一步提升效率，并以牛顿法（Newton's method）作为基准进行比较。

Result: 作者完整求解了2维和3维中至多5个节点的所有刚性线性平移弹簧网络的平衡点，给出了平面弹簧网络最大解数的精确总次数界（total degree bounds），并证明了参数同伦方法在计算效率上显著优于牛顿法基准，结合多面体同伦方法后效率进一步提升。

Conclusion: 非线性代数方法为弹簧网络的平衡分析提供了一套系统且高效的数值框架，参数同伦方法因其物理结构性的天然契合而尤为有效，为更大规模弹簧网络及其他类似物理系统的平衡问题研究奠定了方法基础。

Abstract: We use tools from nonlinear algebra to study the equilibria of small linear translational spring networks. Specifically we use the techniques of homotopy continuation, monodromy, and parameter homotopy (a.k.a. cheater homotopy) to solve all rigid linear translational spring networks up to $5$ nodes in both $2$ and $3$ dimensions. We describe a method of implementing parameter homotopy that arises naturally from the physical structure of the system. We give precise total degree bounds on the maximum number of solutions for general planar spring networks. We discuss further efficiency gains obtained from polyhedral homotopy methods. We compare the computation efficiency of these techniques against a baseline of Newton's method.

</details>


### [187] [Experiments with membranes, maps and sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03152)
*Daniel Holmes,Yannik Schuler*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文受M理论猜想存在性启发，研究了具有环面作用的Calabi-Yau五重态Z上枚举不变量之间的对应关系网，包括五重态Gromov-Witten不变量与三重态X⊂Z的K理论Pandharipande-Thomas不变量之间的对应，由推广Gopakumar-Vafa不变量的膜指标所中介。作者对strip几何和受限环面作用建立了该对应，并在数值上测试了一般环面作用的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于M理论猜想的存在性——通过建立Calabi-Yau五重态上若干枚举不变量之间的Web对应关系网，探索M理论背后的数学结构。这延续了弦论/M理论中由Gopakumar-Vafa不变量在四重态情形所建立的对偶框架，将其推广至五重态情形。

Method: 文章通过"膜指标"(membrane indices)这一推广Gopakumar-Vafa不变量的中介工具，建立五重态Gromov-Witten不变量与三重态的K理论Pandharipande-Thomas不变量之间的对应。对strip几何的受限环面作用给出了严格证明；对其他情形（一般环面作用、closed vertex、局部曲面、局部射影空间）提供数值验证并给出猜想公式；对五重态为仿射平面与合适toric簇乘积的情形给出几何工程化的证明。

Result: 在strip几何的受限环面作用下建立了对应关系；对一般环面作用、closed vertex、局部曲面和局部射影空间提供了数值证据；对局部射影空间给出了低度膜指标的猜想公式；在特殊乘积情形下证明了几何工程化，将Pandharipande-Thomas不变量的生成级数与瞬子配分函数恒等，并与Gromov-Witten系列进行数值对照。

Conclusion: 本文为五重态上的Gromov-Witten/PT对应提供了理论和数值上的重要证据，揭示了膜指标作为统一不变量在不同几何上下文中的中介作用，为进一步探索M理论的数学化表述及其与量子场论配分函数的深层联系奠定了基础。

Abstract: Motivated by the conjectural existence of M-theory, we investigate a web of correspondences between enumerative invariants of a Calabi-Yau fivefold $Z$ with a torus action. This includes a correspondence between the fivefold Gromov-Witten invariants and K-theoretic Pandharipande-Thomas invariants of a threefold $X \subset Z$, mediated by so-called membrane indices, which generalise Gopakumar-Vafa invariants. We establish the correspondence for strip geometries for restricted torus actions and test it numerically for general torus actions. Further numerical evidence is provided for the closed vertex, local surfaces and local projective spaces. For the latter we present conjectural formulae for their low-degree membrane indices. When the fivefold is the product of the affine plane with a suitable toric variety, we prove geometric engineering and equate the generating series of Pandharipande-Thomas invariants with the corresponding instanton partition function while equality with the Gromov-Witten series is probed numerically.

</details>


### [188] [Some generalizations of Oort's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03188)
*Ryosuke Shimada,Teppei Takamatsu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明在素数 p≥5 时，超奇异主极化阿贝尔簇模空间 S_g 中三类特定轨迹（偶维数的某些超奇异 Ekedahl–Oort 层、g≥3 时正 Coxeter 型非超奇异 EO 不变量轨迹、g≥4 时 a-数至少为 2 的轨迹）均包含一个开稠密子概形，其上所有主极化阿贝尔簇的自同构群恰为 {±1}。由此推出对 g≥4，S_g 中自同构群不平凡的开轨迹的补集余维数至少为 2，从而在 p≥5 时证实了 Oort 猜想。证明通过归约到 GSp_{2g} 的仿射 Deligne–Lusztig 簇完成，并对 GL_{2g} 和 GSO_{4m} 证明了类似结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Oort 猜想断言超奇异主极化阿贝尔簇模空间中，自同构群平凡（即 {±1}）的开稠密子轨迹应当存在且其补集有较大余维数，这是研究模空间几何结构与自同构群分布的重要开放问题。作者旨在对 p≥5 证明该猜想，推动对超奇异阿贝尔簇模空间整体结构的理解。

Method: 作者将模空间中自同构群结构的分析归约为 GSp_{2g} 上仿射 Deligne–Lusztig 簇的性质研究，借助仿射 Deligne–Lusztig 簇的维数与不可约性等既有工具，分别对三类 EO（Ekedahl–Oort）轨迹——偶维数的特定超奇异层、正 Coxeter 型非超奇异层（g≥3）以及 a-数至少 2 的轨迹（g≥4）——逐类建立存在开稠密平凡自同构子轨迹。同时推广技术路线，对 GL_{2g} 与 GSO_{4m} 的情形也验证相应命题。

Result: 成功证明：对 p≥5，上述三类轨迹各自包含开稠密子概形且其上的主极化阿贝尔簇自同构群平凡；当 g≥4 时，自同构群非平凡的轨迹补集在 S_g 中余维数至少为 2。该结果在 p≥5 情形下证明了 Oort 猜想，并通过仿射 Deligne–Lusztig 簇归约给出了统一且可推广的证明框架，同时为 GL_{2g} 与 GSO_{4m} 的正 Coxeter 型情境确立了平行结论。

Conclusion: 本文通过仿射 Deligne–Lusztig 簇的方法，在 p≥5 时完整证实了超奇异主极化阿贝尔簇模空间中自同构群平凡（±1）轨迹的存在性与大开稠密性，给出 Oort 猜想的正面解答，并推广到其他经典群结构，深化了对超奇异模空间轨迹几何及其自同构分布规律的理解。

Abstract: For a prime $p\geq 5$, let $\mathscr S_g$ be the moduli space over $\overline{\mathbb F}_p$ of $g$-dimensional principally polarized supersingular abelian varieties. We show that each of the following loci contains an open dense subscheme on which the principally polarized abelian varieties have automorphism group $\{\pm1\}$: (i) certain supersingular Ekedahl--Oort strata when $g$ is even, (ii) the loci in $\mathscr S_g$ with non-supersingular Ekedahl--Oort invariants of positive Coxeter type when $g\geq 3$, and (iii) the locus in $\mathscr S_g$ with $a$-number at least $2$ when $g\geq 4$. Consequently, for $g\geq 4$, the complement in $\mathscr S_g$ of the open locus where the automorphism group is $\{\pm1\}$ has codimension at least $2$. These results confirm Oort's conjecture for $p\geq 5$. We reduce them to statements about affine Deligne--Lusztig varieties for $\operatorname{GSp}_{2g}$ and prove analogues of (ii) for $\operatorname{GL}_{2g}$ and $\operatorname{GSO}_{4m}$.

</details>
